## R2 — §4 + §13 — the end-to-end lifecycle (Describe → … → Run forever) and what the output IS

### R2.0 · The Genesis journey map ticks "Operate — Run · defend · improve · grow" as complete the moment a repo exists, while the entity's own record says autonomous cycles are OFF — **DOC_OVERCLAIM** · tier 1

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** It is the §4.10 "run forever" claim, shown as a completed lifecycle milestone, computed from the mere existence of a generated repo rather than from any cycle having run. The backend already carries the honest string; the milestone does not read it.
- **claim:** §4.10 — "Run · Defend · Heal · Learn · Improve · Grow — forever. The established VSB self-operates and forever evolves on live intelligence, research, and analysis"
- **observed:** apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/synthesis/GenesisJourney.tsx:486 — `{ id: 'operate', label: 'Operate', done: !!vsb && surfaces, hint: 'Run · defend · improve · grow' }`, where line 480 defines `const surfaces = !!(repo || site || webapp || pwa)`. A done step renders a filled highlight tile with a ✓ (lines 495-501). So establishing a VSB and clicking "Generate VSB Repository" is enough to tick Operate. I executed POST /api/v1/genesis/journey (establish=true, ship_output=true) and then GET /api/v1/heartbeat/status: every autonomy lever is off. The comment at line 476-478 claims "Each milestone lights from REAL state (no fabrication) ... operating".
- **evidence:** journey response: "living":{"autonomous_operation":"registered on the living roster — autonomous economy cycles are OFF (the heartbeat's Self-run lever is off), so only the birth cycle ran","autonomous_cycles":false}. GET /api/v1/heartbeat/status: "auto_ship":false,"auto_evolve":false,"auto_economy":false,"auto_align":false,"auto_compliance":false,"last_vsb_operated":null,"last_evolution":null,"last_heal":null. The payload that says nothing operates and the tile that says Operate is done are on the same page.
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). Reproduced exactly, with my own entity, and the surface is worse than the write-up says in two ways.  1. The rail is unconditional and first. The milestone IIFE at apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/synthesis/GenesisJourney.tsx:478-514 sits directly after the header with no `result &&` guard, so it is the first thing on the reached page. Establishing a VSB plus one click of "Generate VSB Repository" ticks every one of the five steps, including Operate, and because `currentIdx = steps.findIndex(s => !s.done)` becomes -1, no step is marked current either: the whole Concept→Commercialisation arc renders as finished. Nothing operated. last_vsb_operated is null, every autonomy lever is off, and the entity's own payload on that same page says so.  2. The tile is logically incapable of being un-ticked. Operate's condition `!!vsb && surfaces` is the conjunction of Establish's condition (`!!vsb`) and Deliver's (`surfaces`). It is therefore true exactly when the two preceding steps are true. The rail can never show "established and delivered, but not yet operating" — the state the organism is actually in, always, by default. This is a done-flag that cannot report the thing it names, which is why the code comment at lines 476-478 ("Each milestone lights from REAL state (no fabrication): described → explored → established → surfaces generated → operating") is false for its last term: the fifth milestone lights from the fourth's state, not from operation.  3. The honest fact exists, is computed, and is ignored. vsb.py:_derived_status already returns one of four values precisely to settle this — "held", "body pending", "registered - not operating", "operating" — and its own docstring records W496/FU-100, where "operational" as a literal was the same defect one layer down. The exact value the tile needs is in the response the page already holds. The page never reads it; grep finds no "autonomous" anywhere in GenesisJourney.tsx.  Not disclosed, so the tier does not come down. The only nearby truth-telling is the status line at line 963 ("vsb-9e841ec4b1 · body pending · intent gate …") plus status_basis at 968, several screens below, past the entire journey output. That line discloses a different fact (body sections awaiting the model) and says nothing about operation; a status line contradicting a check-marked tile is a contradiction on one page, not a disclosure on the claim. The §4.10 claim being ticked is "Run · Defend · Heal · Learn · Improve · Grow — forever", and no part of it has run.  Not the environment. AI_DISABLE_LOCAL=1 explains "body pending"; it does not explain the Operate tick, which is driven by heartbeat levers that are off by default and would tick identically with the model on. Tier 1 holds: a reached surface certifies a lifecycle milestone it did not assess, on a page whose own comment claims no fabrication.
- **refuter's evidence:** My own journey, not the assessor's. POST /api/v1/genesis/journey with my own problem ("A neighbourhood tool library that lends power tools to residents and tracks maintenance of each tool", establish=true, ship_output=true) → HTTP 200, vsb-9e841ec4b1. Its own record: status "body pending", status_basis "4 body section(s) still await the owned model: commercialisation, concept, design, operations", living.autonomous_cycles=false, living.autonomous_operation="registered on the living roster - autonomous economy cycles are OFF (the heartbeat's Self-run lever is off), so only the birth cycle ran". Confirmed live by GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-9e841ec4b1 (same status + living block). GET /api/v1/heartbeat/status: auto_ship/auto_evolve/auto_economy/auto_align/auto_compliance all false, last_vsb_operated null, last_evolution null, last_heal null. Then POST /api/v1/vsb/vsb-9e841ec4b1/repo → HTTP 200, 13 files / 11444 bytes, which is exactly the frontend's `repo` state, so `surfaces` at GenesisJourney.tsx:480 is true and the Operate step's `done: !!vsb && surfaces` (line 486) is true. Read the tile renderer (lines 487-513): a done step gets bg-highlight/10 border-highlight/30, a filled highlight dot with a 'check' glyph and highlight-coloured label, under the hint "Run · defend · improve · grow". Read agentic_core/api/vsb.py:1405-1438 (_derived_status). Grepped the page: no occurrence of "autonomous", "Self-run" in the rail or anywhere the living block is rendered.

### R2.1 · Every established VSB is stamped stage "commercialise" as a hardcoded literal — the cockpit badge and the shipped README print it next to "body pending" — **STUB** · tier 1

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** A lifecycle-stage badge on a reached surface (and in the shipped §13 output) asserts the entity reached the commercialise stage of the §4 lifecycle when its own basis field says its concept, design, operations and commercialisation are all still pending. W496 fixed the colour of the status badge beside it and left this one asserting the strongest stage unconditionally.
- **claim:** §4 — the lifecycle is "One continuous, intelligent, autonomous workflow" of ten numbered stages ending at §4.8 "Establish the VSB IDBO Enterprise"; §13 — the repo is "the enterprise's living body"
- **observed:** agentic_core/api/genesis.py:1125 and :1338 both set `"stage": "commercialise"` as a constant on the entity at birth, on both the blocking and the SSE establish paths. apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/enterprise/VSBCockpit.tsx:599 renders `{detail.stage && <Badge color="aura">{detail.stage}</Badge>}` immediately after the status badge. agentic_core/api/vsb.py:113 writes the same value into the shipped README: `f"- **Stage:** {vsb.get('stage')} · **Status:** {vsb.get('status')}"`. I read GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-078e6b3af2 for the entity my journey established.
- **evidence:** GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-078e6b3af2 → "status":"body pending","stage":"commercialise","body_pending":{"concept":true,"design":true,"commercialisation":true,"operations":true},"status_basis":"4 body section(s) still await the owned model: commercialisation, concept, design, operations", and "genesis_blueprint":{"concept":"content pending the owned model — this enterprise has not yet composed its own concept",...}. The shipped README.md reads: "- **Stage:** commercialise · **Status:** body pending".
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). STANDS as assessed, with evidence stronger than the assessor's, and tier 1 is correct.  The claim is untrue on a reached surface. "commercialise" is not a loose adjective here — the same module defines it as stage 6 of 8 of the §4 Concept-to-Commercialisation lifecycle, "Commercial CoE creates marketplace listing and launch plan", and serves that definition to the UI through /review-gates while validating review-gate ids against the same list. So the string the README labels "**Stage:**" is drawn from the lifecycle vocabulary and reads as a lifecycle position. My SSE entity vsb-69df699437 had an empty concept, empty stage_verifications, generation 0, autonomous cycles off ("only the birth cycle ran"), and no marketplace listing or launch plan of any kind — and its shipped §13 body still asserts it is at Commercialisation. Nothing after intake ran.  My own input makes the case tighter than the assessor's in two ways they did not have:  First, it isolates the constant. Their entity reported status "body pending" with body_pending all true; both of mine report "registered - not operating" with body_pending all false. The `status` field therefore demonstrably derives — three entities, two different values. The `stage` field is byte-identical across all three. It is a literal, not a computation, and the grep confirms no writer anywhere ever advances it: an entity is stamped "commercialise" at birth and can never leave it, so the field can never become true and can never be falsified. That is the STUB verdict precisely — the lifecycle machinery around it is real (8 stages, per-stage review gates, stage_verifications, gate decisions), but the entity's own stage has zero connection to any of it.  Second, it covers both code paths with distinct inputs, so this is not an artefact of one request shape.  Tier 1 holds, and the "does the surface disclose it" test fails. W496 added `status_basis`, which both the cockpit (data-testid cockpit-status-basis) and the README's Status field carry — and it explains the STATUS only. There is no `stage_basis`, no caveat, and nothing anywhere says the stage value is unconditional. The cockpit prints the stage as a bare aura Badge with no label at all, immediately after the status badge and its basis span, which borrows the credibility of the one derived field next to it. The README prints it under an explicit "**Stage:**" label in a file on disk, so the claim travels with the shipped output and outlives the page — the "a badge in the DOM is not a label on the text" class, where disclosure must accompany the artefact. Adjacency to a contradicting status ("body pending", "registered - not operating") is a contradiction a reader must notice, not a disclosure.  This is also not the environment exemption. AI_DISABLE_LOCAL=1 explains why the body sections are unwritten; it does not explain a hardcoded lifecycle stage, which would read "commercialise" identically with every model running. The defect is the unconditional literal, not the absent model.  I considered two routes to MILDER and rejected both. (a) That `stage` is really a scope: `scope` is set to the same literal in the same dict, and vsb.py:1965 does "stage": req.scope elsewhere, so the author may have meant breadth rather than position. But the reached surface says "Stage", and the value is a member of the lifecycle id list — under "a field's meaning belongs to its readers", the surface's label and the shared vocabulary decide the meaning, not the author's intent. (b) That the neighbouring Status line discloses enough: it reports operating state, a different claim, and it does not mention that the stage was never assessed.  One narrowing for accuracy, which does not change verdict or tier: "commercialise" is stage 6 of 8 in the canon, not the terminal stage — the assessor's "ten numbered stages ending at §4.8" is the vision's numbering, while the code's own list ends at genome then launch. The literal names a mid-lifecycle stage, which is if anything odder than stamping the last one.
- **refuter's evidence:** I established TWO new VSBs of my own choosing on the live backend, one per code path, and read the stage off each.  (1) Blocking path — POST /api/v1/genesis/establish with my own input ("masjid water-recycling for wudu ablution reuse", domain environment, realm planet, concept/design/commercialisation all supplied) → vsb-a4b796dea0. GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-a4b796dea0 → "stage":"commercialise", "status":"registered - not operating", body_pending all FALSE.  (2) SSE path (the :1338 site) — POST /api/v1/genesis/establish/stream, "date-palm offcut biochar for arid soil", domain agriculture, concept/design/commercialisation deliberately EMPTY → vsb-69df699437. GET → "stage":"commercialise", genesis_blueprint.concept == "" (empty string), genesis_journey.stage_verifications == {}.  (3) Read the SHIPPED README for each from its own repo root (GET /api/v1/vsb/{id}/repo gives repo_root; both shipped, initial_ship {"shipped":true,"commit":"edbd68c67eb9"} and {"shipped":true,"commit":"29ea9e235a05"}). Both files line 6, verbatim:   "- **Stage:** commercialise · **Status:** registered - not operating"  (4) Read the source: agentic_core/api/genesis.py:1125 "stage": "commercialise", and :1338 "status": "pending derivation", "stage": "commercialise" — both literals, both with the W496/FU-100 comment covering only `status`. apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/enterprise/VSBCockpit.tsx:599 {detail.stage && <Badge color="aura">{detail.stage}</Badge>}, and App.tsx:191 routes /vsb-cockpit, so the badge is reached. agentic_core/api/vsb.py:113 writes the README line.  (5) Established the vocabulary: agentic_core/api/vsb.py:64 _CONCEPT_TO_COMMERCIALISE_STAGES — "commercialise" is stage 6 of 8, name "Commercialisation", description "Commercial CoE creates marketplace listing and launch plan". GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-a4b796dea0/review-gates serves that exact id/name/description back as `lifecycle`, and _LIFECYCLE_STAGE_IDS (vsb.py:1367) validates gate stage ids against it.  (6) Grepped every writer of an entity's stage across agentic_core: only genesis.py:1125 and :1338. No route anywhere advances it after birth.

### R2.2 · The repository card prints "9 files · 10656 bytes" and a 9-path tree for a repository that holds 30 files and 87,171 bytes — omitting every Website, Web-app and Phone-app file — **PARTIAL** · tier 1

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** Two figures a person reads as the size of their delivered enterprise repository are wrong by a factor of three (files) and eight (bytes), and the tree beneath them silently drops the three surfaces §13 names as the canonical output's integrated body.
- **claim:** §13 — "The canonical output is a Repository of the IDBO Entity (VSB) ... shipped as a coherent, version-controlled whole that integrates a Website, a Web app, and a Phone (mobile) app as the entity's public + operational surfaces."
- **observed:** agentic_core/api/vsb.py:408-411 builds the manifest from `written`, the list the repo-body generator itself wrote in that call: `"file_count": len(written), "total_bytes": sum(...), "tree": sorted(x["path"] for x in written)`. The website/webapp/mobile generators write their own files and never update this manifest, and vsb.py:2030-2032 runs the repo generator FIRST in the ship loop. GenesisJourney.tsx:1014 renders `{repo.file_count} files · {repo.total_bytes} bytes` and :1025 renders `{repo.tree.join('  ·  ')}`. I walked the generated repo on disk and compared it to its own manifest.json.
- **evidence:** On disk: 30 files, 87,171 bytes. manifest.json / GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-078e6b3af2/repo: "file_count": 9, "total_bytes": 10656, tree = ['BUSINESS_PLAN.md','EVIDENCE.md','IDENTITY.md','OPERATIONS.md','ORGANISATION.md','README.md','compliance/QUALITY.md','genome.json','resources/cascades.json']. Absent from the tree: web/index.html, web/about.html, web/solution.html, web/styles.css, web/site.json, webapp/index.html, webapp/app.js, webapp/styles.css, webapp/app.json, webapp/data.json, mobile/index.html, mobile/app.js, mobile/sw.js, mobile/manifest.webmanifest, mobile/icon.svg, mobile/styles.css, mobile/data.json, mobile/app.json, mobile/README.md, webapp/README.md, manifest.json. On the FIRST ship the same figures read 13 files / 11535 bytes with all three surfaces labelled "(scaffold)".
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). Reproduced on my own independently chosen entity, so the premise is not specific to the assessor's VSB — it is structural in how the manifest is built.  Tier 1 holds, and the second ship is the strongest case. The Repository card is a reached surface: it prints "9 files · 7642 bytes" and a nine-path tree, and the line immediately beneath it (:1026) reads "Integrated surfaces — Website: web/ (generated — index · about · solution) · Web app: webapp/ (generated) · Phone app: mobile/ (generated PWA)". So the card asserts in one breath that all three §13 surfaces were generated and in the next prints a file count and a body that contain none of them. Nothing on the card qualifies the figures as repo-body-only: the manifest's `note` field is never rendered (no `repo.note` reference exists in the page), and no adjacent text says the count excludes the surfaces. A person reads those two numbers as the size of their delivered enterprise repository and they are wrong by 3.3x in files and 11x in bytes. This is not the AI_DISABLE_LOCAL environment — the files genuinely exist on disk with real content; it is an accounting surface that under-reports its own output.  Two corrections to the finding's wording, neither changing direction: (1) "omitting every Website, Web-app and Phone-app file" is not true of the FIRST ship — the repo generator writes four of its own surface scaffolds (web/index.html, webapp/README.md, mobile/README.md, mobile/manifest.webmanifest), so four surface paths do appear in the 13-file tree. The total omission happens only on re-ship, which is precisely when the surfaces line claims "(generated)". That sharpens the finding rather than softening it. (2) A consequence the assessor did not name: the figure moves the WRONG WAY as the repository becomes more complete. My total_bytes fell from 8577 to 7642 and file_count from 13 to 9 between ship 1 and ship 2, while the disk stayed at 30 files, because the repo generator stops emitting its scaffolds once the real surfaces exist. The number a founder watches shrinks as their repository grows.  The wrong figures are also written into manifest.json on disk (14,414 bytes, itself uncounted), so the mis-statement travels with the downloaded artifact, not just the page.  Verdict PARTIAL is right: §13's integrated body is really generated — 30 files, all three surfaces, real content — and the defect is the unified manifest that reports it, not missing capability. Fix is to recompute file_count/total_bytes/tree from a walk of the repo root (or to have the ship route re-aggregate after all five generators run) rather than from the first generator's `written` list, and to keep the card's count and its "(generated)" claim derived from the same walk.
- **refuter's evidence:** I spawned my OWN VSB on the live backend — vsb-f2eb9f5037 "Khalas Cold Chain Cooperative" (agriculture, waqf_ltd_hybrid, date-palm cold-chain challenge), not the assessor's VarroaGuard — then POSTed /api/v1/vsb/vsb-f2eb9f5037/repo/ship twice and walked the generated repo root on disk against its own manifest.json and against GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-f2eb9f5037/repo.  SHIP 1 — manifest/card: "13 files · 8577 bytes"; disk: 30 files, 83,807 bytes. integrated_surfaces all read "(scaffold)". SHIP 2 — manifest/card: "9 files · 7642 bytes", tree = BUSINESS_PLAN.md · EVIDENCE.md · IDENTITY.md · OPERATIONS.md · ORGANISATION.md · README.md · compliance/QUALITY.md · genome.json · resources/cascades.json; disk: 30 files, 83,610 bytes. 17 real files on disk are absent from the rendered tree: web/about.html, web/solution.html, web/styles.css, web/site.json, webapp/index.html, webapp/app.js, webapp/styles.css, webapp/app.json, webapp/data.json, mobile/index.html, mobile/app.js, mobile/sw.js, mobile/styles.css, mobile/icon.svg, mobile/data.json, mobile/app.json, manifest.json. Zero tree entries were missing from disk.  Code read: agentic_core/api/vsb.py:397-410 builds the manifest from `written`, the repo-body list of that one call (`"file_count": len(written)`, `"total_bytes": sum(x["bytes"] ...)`, `"tree": sorted(x["path"] ...)`). The website (:682), webapp (:883) and mobile (:1020-1021) generators each compute their own separate counts into their own store files (`{id}.website.json` etc.) and never touch `{id}.manifest.json`; vsb.py:2030-2032 runs the repo generator first in the ship tuple. GET /repo (:435-453) returns that manifest untouched except for ship_status. apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/synthesis/GenesisJourney.tsx:1014 renders `{repo.file_count} files · {repo.total_bytes} bytes` and :1025 renders `{repo.tree.join('  ·  ')}`, populated by setRepo at :359.

### R2.3 · The §4 lifecycle stage rail marks all six stages complete on a journey the backend stopped at Stage 5 — **DOC_OVERCLAIM** · tier 1

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** The surface tells the user three lifecycle stages completed in the same render where the payload says they never ran and nothing was designed or commercialised.
- **claim:** §4 — the ten-stage lifecycle, each stage "modelled, simulated, optimised, categorised and ranked, and ... verified, tested and validated"
- **observed:** apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/synthesis/GenesisJourney.tsx:526 — `const done = !!result;` inside the card headed "Concept → Commercialisation · fine-resolution lifecycle (§4)" (line 515). Every one of the six tiles (Conceptualise, Innovate & Research, Model·Simulate·Rank, Design & Development, Operational Intelligence, Commercialise) takes its completed styling from that one flag, with no reference to `result.status`, `blocked_by_screen` or `stage_verifications`. The page handles blocked state correctly elsewhere (lines 770-780, 899-910) but not here. I proved the blocked path by executing a journey the §11 screen vetoes.
- **evidence:** POST /api/v1/genesis/journey with a riba/gambling/alcohol problem returned: "status":"blocked_by_screen", "deliverable":"No deliverable — the §11 screen vetoed every candidate, so the journey stopped after Stage 5 and nothing was designed or commercialised", vetoed ['pragmatic','innovative','lean'], selected null, and stage_verifications design/operations/commercialisation each "ran": false, "basis":"not run — the journey was blocked by the §11 screen before this stage, so there is nothing to verify". With that response in state, `done = !!result` is true, so Design & Development, Operational Intelligence and Commercialise all render as completed.
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). STANDS, SAME direction, tier 1 holds.  The code is verbatim as assessed and I reproduced the blocked backend state with my own journey, so the premise is sound: with a blocked payload in state, `done` is true for all six §4 tiles and the three stages the backend says never ran take the same completed styling as the three that did.  On tier. The rule is that tier 1 needs a reached surface telling a user something untrue, and that a surface which discloses the shortfall drops to tier 2/3. The rail does not disclose. It contains no reference to status, no veto note, no qualifier - the blocked state is disclosed only further down the page, at the Stage 5 card (770-780), in the deliverable sentence (817) and on the disabled Establish button (899-910). The rail sits ABOVE the input card, so the untrue completion state is what the user sees first and the correction requires scrolling past the form. "A badge elsewhere in the DOM is not a label on this text" applies squarely: the rail is its own surface and carries none of the truth the payload states.  Two honest qualifications, neither of which moves the tier: (1) The rail tiles signal completion by STYLING only - bg-highlight/10, border-highlight/20, highlight icon, white label - with no ✓ glyph and no word "complete". Taken alone that is a weaker claim than a sentence, and the finding's title ("marks all six stages complete") is slightly stronger than the rail's literal content. But highlight is unambiguously this page's done state: the step pills immediately above use the same highlight tokens and print ✓ for done, slate for not-started, pulsing aura for running. A user reading the page's own vocabulary reads six completed stages. (2) The stronger instance is the one the assessor missed: line 482/500 prints an actual ✓ on the "Explore" milestone whose hint reads "Conceptualise · Design · Commercialise", from the identical `!!result` flag. That is a glyph plus text asserting completion of Design and Commercialise on a run where stage_verifications says both ran=false. That alone is a tier-1 untrue surface, so the class is tier 1 even on the most conservative reading of the rail's colour-only signal.  This is not the environment. AI_DISABLE_LOCAL=1 shaped HOW I had to reach the veto - the floor's frequency summariser strips low-frequency haram tokens, which is why my first, more natural haram problem statement passed the screen - but the defect is independent of it: the fault is a UI flag that ignores a status field the backend sets correctly and that the same page reads correctly in three other places. The backend is right; only the surface lies. DOC_OVERCLAIM is the correct verdict label.  Worth flagging to the parent beyond the finding: run 1 is a separate, unreported weakness - a plainly haram venture ("usurious interest", "pork futures", "wine bar") sailed through the §11 screen to status "complete" with sharia_halal only "review", because the screened text is the floor's top-terms summary rather than the user's problem. The screen is being applied to a lossy paraphrase of the subject. That is a different finding from R2.3 and I did not assess its tier.
- **refuter's evidence:** I executed POST /api/v1/genesis/journey on the live 127.0.0.1:8086 backend TWICE with my OWN inputs, not the assessor's riba/gambling/alcohol text.  Run 1 (my first attempt, a deliberately different subject): "Launch a pawnbroking chain that charges compound usurious interest on microloans secured against pork futures, with a slot-machine lounge and a wine bar in every branch..." -> HTTP 200, status "complete", vetoed [], selected "pragmatic". NOT blocked. Reading the payload showed why: _screen_candidate screens c["approach"], and under AI_DISABLE_LOCAL=1 the floor composes the approach from the request's most-frequent terms only ("interest microloans", "slot-machine lounge", "launch pawnbroking"), so "pork", "wine" and "usurious" ("usury\w*" does not match "usurious") never reached the screen text. sharia_halal came back "review", disqualified false. So the blocked path is NOT reachable from an arbitrary haram problem statement - the haram token must survive the floor's frequency summariser.  Run 2 (my own veto, constructed from that fact, casino/lottery/pork rather than riba/gambling/alcohol): problem text repeating "casino lottery" and "pork bacon lard" so those tokens dominate the extracted terms -> HTTP 200 and:   status = "blocked_by_screen"   deliverable = "No deliverable - the §11 screen vetoed every candidate, so the journey stopped after Stage 5 and nothing was designed or commercialised"   stage_5.vetoed = ['pragmatic','innovative','lean'], selected = None, blocked_by_screen = True   stage_verifications design / operations / commercialisation / implementation / economics each ran=false, basis "not run - the journey was blocked by the §11 screen before this stage, so there is nothing to verify" That is the assessor's state, produced by my own independently chosen journey.  Then I read the surface. apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/synthesis/GenesisJourney.tsx:   - line 515 Card opens, headed "Concept → Commercialisation · fine-resolution lifecycle (§4)" (line 516); line 541 closes it; line 544 is the Input card. The rail Card is top-level JSX with NO `{result && ...}` guard, so it renders on every visit and its state flips the moment a result arrives.   - line 526 `const done = !!result;` inside the .map over all six tiles. Verbatim as assessed. No reference to result.status, stage_5.blocked_by_screen or stage_verifications anywhere between 515 and 541 (I grepped the whole file: result.status appears only at 899/900/905/909, blocked_by_screen only at 28/770/776/780).   - line 224-226: `if (!res.ok) {...return;} setResult(data);` - my run returned HTTP 200, so setResult(data) runs with the blocked payload and done === true for Design & Development, Operational Intelligence and Commercialise.   - I also found a SECOND instance of the same flag that the assessor did not report and that is stronger evidence: line 482, the whole-journey progress map, `{ id:'explore', label:'Explore', done: !!result, hint:'Conceptualise · Design · Commercialise' }`, and line 500 renders `{s.done ? '✓' : i + 1}`. So on my blocked run the page prints a literal ✓ on a milestone whose own hint names Design and Commercialise.

### R2.4 · The shipped repository tells the founder its Website, Web app and Phone app are scaffolds "NOT built/compiled/running apps" — in the same manifest whose computed field says "(generated PWA)" — **DOC_OVERCLAIM** · tier 1

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** The §13 canonical output's own README and manifest — the two files a founder opens first — assert a falsehood about the three surfaces §13 names, and the manifest contradicts itself in a single response. An untrue statement in the shipped artefact is a truth defect whichever direction it errs.
- **claim:** §13 (W446 recorded scope) — "the Website is a real multi-page static site, the Web app a real client-side interactive app, the Phone app a real installable PWA (offline-capable when hosted)"
- **observed:** agentic_core/api/vsb.py:421-423 writes a CONSTANT `note` into every manifest: "...web/webapp/mobile are scaffolds for later increments (NOT built/compiled/running apps)". vsb.py:125 writes a constant README line: "- `web/`, `webapp/`, `mobile/` — integrated Website / Web app / Phone app (scaffold)". Both are unconditional, while vsb.py:412-415 computes `integrated_surfaces` from disk. I re-shipped and read the manifest back: the two statements contradict each other in one response. I then read the files to establish which is true.
- **evidence:** GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-078e6b3af2/repo after a re-ship: integrated_surfaces = {'website': 'web/ (generated — index · about · solution)', 'webapp': 'webapp/ (generated)', 'mobile': 'mobile/ (generated PWA)'} while note = "...web/webapp/mobile are scaffolds for later increments (NOT built/compiled/running apps)." On disk web/ holds index.html + about.html + solution.html + styles.css with a working 3-item nav; webapp/app.js is a real 2.6 KB tabbed client-side app that fetches data.json; mobile/sw.js is a real service worker (`caches.open(CACHE)`, install/activate/fetch handlers) beside manifest.webmanifest and icon.svg.
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). STANDS, tier 1 confirmed, on my own VSB. The defect is a constant string asserted against computed state. vsb.py:420-423 writes the `note` unconditionally, and vsb.py:125 writes the README "(scaffold)" line unconditionally, while vsb.py:412-415 computes integrated_surfaces from `_generated` (vsb.py:363-365, probing web/site.json, webapp/app.json, mobile/app.json on disk). So a single GET response says "generated PWA" and "NOT built/compiled/running apps" at once, and the files I fetched over HTTP contradict the second: three linked HTML pages with a shared stylesheet, a 2.6 KB client-side app that fetches data.json, and a working service worker + webmanifest + icon. Nothing anywhere discloses the shortfall or scopes the note to the ungenerated case — I checked all four other manifests and the note is byte-identical whether the surfaces are generated or really scaffolds, which is exactly what makes it a basis string rather than a reading. That is a reached surface (HTTP 200 route, plus the README a founder opens first) telling the user something untrue about the three §13 surfaces, so tier 1 holds; it errs toward under-claiming rather than over-claiming, but an untrue statement in the shipped artefact is a truth defect in either direction, and the verdict enum offers no DOC_UNDERCLAIM, so DOC_OVERCLAIM is the right bucket. THREE THINGS THE ASSESSOR MISSED, all of which matter for the fix: (1) webapp/README.md ("Web app (scaffold)… built in a later increment (not a compiled app)") and mobile/README.md ("Phone app (scaffold)… not a compiled app") are STILL ON DISK beside the generated apps — two more untrue writers at vsb.py:216-220. (2) The reason they survive is a real gap in the W450 pop logic: the loop at vsb.py:366-369 only drops a scaffold from the in-memory `files` dict when app.json already exists; it never deletes a scaffold file written on an earlier pass. On my VSB the mtimes prove the order — web/webapp/mobile README.md at 05:35 (repo step), the generated app files at 05:36 — so the stale scaffold text persists permanently. (3) Because those paths are popped from `files`, the manifest's own `tree` lists only the 9 doc files and reports file_count 9 / total_bytes 7642, omitting all 20 generated surface files on disk — so the manifest both calls the surfaces scaffolds and refuses to list them. Fixing only the two lines the assessor cited would leave two untrue files shipped and the tree still blind. The fix must (a) derive the note and the README structure line from `_generated` instead of asserting a constant, (b) rewrite or delete the per-surface scaffold READMEs when a surface is generated, and (c) include the generated surface files in tree/file_count/total_bytes.
- **refuter's evidence:** I did NOT use the assessor's vsb-078e6b3af2. I listed GET /api/v1/vsb (5 entities) and chose my own: vsb-f2eb9f5037 "Khalas Cold Chain Cooperative". GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-f2eb9f5037/repo returned, in ONE response, integrated_surfaces = {website: "web/ (generated — index · about · solution)", webapp: "webapp/ (generated)", mobile: "mobile/ (generated PWA)"} AND note = "Bespoke VSB IDBO entity repository — real scaffold files generated in-house from the entity's own data; web/webapp/mobile are scaffolds for later increments (NOT built/compiled/running apps)." I then read the repo_root on disk (…/scratchpad/m1/vsb_repos/vsb-f2eb9f5037): web/ = index.html 1336B + about.html 1124B + solution.html 1024B + styles.css + site.json; webapp/ = index.html + app.js 2596B + data.json 3445B + styles.css + app.json; mobile/ = index.html + app.js 2596B + sw.js 541B (real service worker: `caches.open(CACHE)`, install/activate/fetch handlers, skipWaiting, clients.claim) + manifest.webmanifest + icon.svg. I executed the serving routes to prove they RUN: GET /vsb-f2eb9f5037/website/page/about → 200 text/html 1124B; /webapp/page/app.js → 200 text/javascript 2568B; /mobile/page/sw.js → 200 text/javascript 536B; /mobile/page/manifest.webmanifest → 200 application/manifest+json 521B. I read the shipped README.md on disk: "- `web/`, `webapp/`, `mobile/` — integrated Website / Web app / Phone app (scaffold)". I read vsb.py:125 (unconditional README line), vsb.py:420-423 (unconditional `note`), vsb.py:412-415 (integrated_surfaces computed from disk via vsb.py:363-365 `_generated`), and vsb.py:366-369 (the pop loop). I also checked the other three VSBs' manifests: all four carry the identical constant note regardless of whether their surfaces are generated or genuine scaffolds.

### R2.5 · The living-deliverable chip claims producing it "expended metabolic ATP" and prints a percentage the same payload says is a simulation that cannot fall — **DOC_OVERCLAIM** · tier 1

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** A figure the system itself says is not a reading of anything is presented to the user as a measured metabolic cost of producing their deliverable. The honest basis string exists in the payload and the surface overrides it with the opposite claim.
- **claim:** §13 — "Not static artefacts but living, specific, optimised, continually-developing, learning ... enterprises"
- **observed:** apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/Deliverables.tsx:269 — title={`§8→§13 — this living deliverable was produced under the organism's homeostatic posture; producing it expended metabolic ATP (ATP ${Math.round((selected.homeostasis.organism?.atp_ratio ?? 0) * 100)}%, ${selected.homeostasis.organism?.circadian}).`}. I executed POST /api/v1/deliverables/produce for the journey's text and read the stored homeostasis block the chip renders from.
- **evidence:** POST /api/v1/deliverables/produce → deliv-a206edb8, homeostasis.organism = {"atp_ratio":0.688,"atp_measured":false,"atp_can_fall":false,"atp_basis":"...production (0.5 x efficiency...) always exceeds consumption (0.1 x load), so within that domain it only rises — from 0.333 at boot to 1.0 ... It is a simulation, not a reading of this platform's energy.","conserving_posture_reachable":false}. The tooltip therefore reads "producing it expended metabolic ATP (ATP 69%, MAINTENANCE_REST)" over a quantity the payload states was not measured and cannot be expended.
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). STANDS, SAME direction, tier 1 confirmed - and my own input makes it worse, not milder.  1. The surface is REACHED, with my own data. Both JSX gates are satisfied by a deliverable I created myself, on the GET-detail path the page uses after selection (not just the produce response). The chip is not behind a dead conditional.  2. The surface says something untrue. The tooltip asserts two things: that producing this deliverable "expended metabolic ATP", and a figure for it. The same payload states atp_measured=false and atp_can_fall=false, and atp_basis states in words that the quantity only RISES and is "a simulation, not a reading of this platform's energy". With my input the figure came back 1.0, i.e. the tooltip reads "expended metabolic ATP (ATP 100%)" - the claim of expenditure is made over a quantity sitting at its own ceiling, which is internally incoherent as well as unmeasured. The assessor's 69% run and my 100% run both contradict the "expended" verb; mine is the starker instance.  3. The surface does NOT disclose it, and the omission is structural rather than incidental. The page's own TypeScript type drops atp_measured, atp_basis and atp_can_fall, so the honest qualifier cannot reach this chip even accidentally. This is the badge-in-the-DOM / qualifier-must-travel-with-the-figure class: homeostasis.py:101-107 was deliberately changed to carry the qualifier alongside the figure ("the qualifier TRAVELS WITH THE FIGURE... this reader dropped both, so every surface downstream printed the number as a live vital"), and biobus.py:85-87 names ResourceFabric.tsx as a page that does carry atp_basis to the viewer. NativeAI.tsx carries it too. Deliverables.tsx is the surface where the fix did not land - the fix-every-writer miss, not a false positive.  4. The environment defence does not apply. atp_can_fall comes from atp_depletion_state() in biobus.py:59-62, which computes can_deplete = (0.1*1.0) > (0.5*0.8) from hardcoded constants. No branch consults AI_DISABLE_LOCAL. The ATP simulator runs the same with local models enabled; the figure would still be unmeasured and still unable to fall. So "a model did not run" is not the cause - the surface is overriding a basis string the backend went out of its way to publish.  5. Tier stays 1, not 2 or 3. The tier-1 test is a reached surface telling a user something untrue or fabricating a figure. This fabricates a figure's MEANING: it converts a simulated, non-falling label into a stated metabolic cost of producing the user's own deliverable, and prints a percentage for it. Nothing adjacent on the page qualifies it. The only reason to consider softening is that the false claim lives in a title attribute (hover) while the visible label "S8 homeostasis: reduced" is defensible on its own - but a tooltip is text the user reads on the element, and this project already treats a printed line and a card footer as surfaces, so that does not move it off tier 1.  DOC_OVERCLAIM is the right verdict label: the homeostatic machinery and the API are real and honest; it is the claim at the UI layer that exceeds what was built and measured.
- **refuter's evidence:** I produced my OWN deliverable rather than re-reading the assessor's: POST /api/v1/deliverables/produce with my own brief ("R2.5 independent refuter probe", a beekeeping cold-chain / varroa brood-break brief) -> deliv-baff247a. I then re-read it through GET /api/v1/deliverables/deliv-baff247a, the exact route the page's selectDeliverable uses (Deliverables.tsx:95), and checked both render gates in that payload: quality_assurance.quality present (the chip container, line ~219) and homeostasis.posture == "reduced" (the chip itself, line 267). My payload's homeostasis.organism = {"atp_ratio": 1.0, "atp_measured": false, "atp_can_fall": false, "conserving_posture_reachable": false, "circadian": "MAINTENANCE_REST"} with atp_basis "...production (0.5 x efficiency...) always exceeds consumption (0.1 x load), so within that domain it only rises - from 0.333 at boot to 1.0 - and the thresholds written against it cannot fire. It is a simulation, not a reading of this platform's energy." Substituting into the template at Deliverables.tsx:269 the tooltip my deliverable renders is: "S8->S13 - this living deliverable was produced under the organism's homeostatic posture; producing it expended metabolic ATP (ATP 100%, MAINTENANCE_REST)." I then grepped the whole page for any qualifier: the Deliverable TS type at Deliverables.tsx:29 declares homeostasis as only { posture?: string; organism?: { atp_ratio?: number; circadian?: string } } - atp_measured, atp_basis and atp_can_fall are not in the type at all - and "simulat" appears nowhere in the file. For contrast I read apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/developers/NativeAI.tsx:651/667/669, which does render atp_can_fall, uses atp_basis as the tooltip and prints " . simulated" when atp_measured is false. Finally I read agentic_core/organism/biobus.py:59-62 and agentic_core/ai/native/homeostasis.py:62-123 to test the environment defence.

### R2.6 · On a blocked journey the stage-verification chips render "NaN%" where a score belongs, inside a card headed "each stage tested & validated" — **PARTIAL** · tier 2

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** The honest state ("not run") exists in the payload and the chip has a slot for it; what the reader gets instead is a broken figure, so the surface does not say that three stages never ran.
- **claim:** §4 preamble — each stage "is verified, tested and validated"
- **observed:** agentic_core/api/genesis.py:610-612 defines `_NOT_RUN = {"verified": None, "checks": "none", "ran": False, "basis": "not run — the journey was blocked by the §11 screen before this stage..."}` — with NO `score` key — and lines 616-618 substitute it for design/operations/commercialisation when blocked. GenesisJourney.tsx:683 renders `{v.verified === null ? '—' : ...} {stage} · {Math.round(v.score * 100)}%` unconditionally, and the TS type at line 47 declares `score: number` (non-optional), so the compiler never saw the gap.
- **evidence:** The blocked journey I executed returned "design": {"verified": null, "checks": "none", "ran": false, "basis": "not run — the journey was blocked by the §11 screen before this stage, so there is nothing to verify"} — no score field. `Math.round(undefined * 100)` is NaN, so the chip reads "— design · NaN%" under the heading "Stage verification — each stage tested & validated (§5)".
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). REPRODUCED, and broader than assessed (5 NaN chips, not 3 — the derived-stage loop at genesis.py:624-626 substitutes the same `_NOT_RUN` dict, so every derived stage is hit too). The machinery is real: _verify_stage runs honest proxies, the not-run state with its reason is in the payload, and the chip has a slot for it ("—") — the page just prints a broken number in the score slot. PARTIAL stands.  I keep tier 2 rather than raising it. "NaN%" is not a fabricated figure and certifies nothing: NaN is self-evidently not a score, the chip prefix is the slate "—" not-assessable marker, the counter reads "0/0 verified" (slate, not green per the W460 fix), the chip's own `title` tooltip carries the full "not run — the journey was blocked by the §11 screen" basis, and the same page renders a vital-bordered role="status" banner "Blocked by the §11 screen / every candidate was vetoed by the §11 screen (pragmatic, innovative, lean), so no approach was selected and the journey stops here. Nothing was designed, commercialised or established." (GenesisJourney.tsx:776-783, blocked_reason verified live). So the stop IS disclosed where the reader is. Nor is it tier 3 cosmetic: the broken figure sits in the one card that claims §5 verification, so the §5 surface is unreadable for every stage that did not run.  SEPARATE FINDING I reproduced while checking the disclosure, which IS a tier-1 candidate and is NOT what R2.6 describes — report it on its own: on the same blocked run the backend returned stages_floor_served: 7 and stages_note "7 of 7 stages were served by the deterministic floor and are not assessable — the verification proxies cannot fail on floor output, so no verdict is claimed for them". Only concept and research were floor-served; the other five were never called at all. genesis.py:730-734 computes both figures as `len(stage_verifications) - stages_assessable`, which lumps `_NOT_RUN` in with floor-served, and the page prints both — the note at GenesisJourney.tsx:657-658 and "· 7 floor-served, not assessable" at lines 673-675. That is a reached surface asserting as fact that five stages were served and measured by the floor when no text was composed for them: the W491 class (a nature/absence field reporting an outcome) recurring. The honest NaN fix and this mislabel share one root — `_NOT_RUN` has no score and no distinct counter — so a fix that only adds a score key would leave the false "floor-served" sentence standing.
- **refuter's evidence:** I executed my OWN blocked journey against the live backend — POST http://127.0.0.1:8086/api/v1/genesis/journey with problem "Launch an online casino and sports betting lottery platform for retail players, funded by interest-bearing consumer credit" (my own §11 trigger, not the assessor's). It returned status "blocked_by_screen", stages_verified "0/0". The payload's stage_verifications carried NO `score` key on FIVE stages, not three: design, operations, commercialisation AND the two derived stages implementation ("derived from the text: it contains 'launch'") and economics ("...contains 'fund'"), each with {"verified": null, "checks": "none", "ran": false, "basis": "not run — the journey was blocked by the §11 screen before this stage, so there is nothing to verify"}. I then evaluated the EXACT chip expression from the page (apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/synthesis/GenesisJourney.tsx:683, `{v.verified === null ? '—' : ...} {stage} · {Math.round(v.score * 100)}%`) over that live payload under node. Output: "— concept · 86%", "— research · 75%", "— design · NaN%", "— operations · NaN%", "— commercialisation · NaN%", "— implementation · NaN%", "— economics · NaN%". I also confirmed the card is reached: it is gated only on `result.stage_verifications` (line 665), which is always present, under the heading "Stage verification — each stage tested & validated (§5)" (line 668); the TS type at line 47 declares `score: number` non-optional, so tsc never saw the gap; and there is no NaN guard anywhere in the page or tests. Tree clean at e0d13ab1. Note: the assessor's file path for the frontend was wrong — the page is at apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/synthesis/GenesisJourney.tsx, not frontend/src/pages/.

### R2.7 · "Innovate & Research" consults no source at all — the gateway is called with augment=False, so §4.3's five disciplines are composed from the request's own term counts — **PARTIAL** · tier 3 *(assessed tier 2)*

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** Real machinery exists and the body text is honest about being a term count, but the card's own §4.3 heading asserts cross-disciplinary discovery and does not say that no source is consulted even in principle (augmentation is disabled by construction, not by the floor).
- **claim:** §4.3 — "Innovate & Research — discover the best, latest, most advanced and effective approaches across science, technology, business, operations, law, and the relevant domain; generate and evaluate innovative options."
- **observed:** agentic_core/api/genesis.py:281 — every journey stage runs through `await gateway.query_meta(_realm_prefix + prompt, agent=agent, augment=False)`; augmentation is switched off for all eight stages, so no corpus, knowledge base or retrieval feeds Stage 3. GenesisJourney.tsx:712-713 still headlines the card "Innovate & Research (§4.3)" with "Best & latest approaches + innovative options across science · technology · business · operations · law — feeding the candidate solutions below." The body text does disclose its nature, and the provenance card above it discloses the floor.
- **evidence:** stage_3_innovate_research from my executed journey, in full at its head: "## SOCH\n The list below is the request's most frequent terms, not findings about them.\n- beekeepers yorkshire\n- yorkshire lose\n- third their\n- their hives\n- each winter". ai_provenance: {"served_by":{"native":13},"any_external":false}. Nothing was discovered; the five disciplines named on the card were never consulted.
- **refutation: REFUTED — corrected to PARTIAL.** The premise is wrong. `augment` is not corpus, knowledge-base or retrieval augmentation: gateway.py:288 is `augmented = self._augment(prompt, owner_id=owner_id) if augment else prompt`, and `_augment` (gateway.py:199) does exactly one thing — `memory.query_memory(prompt, owner_id=owner_id)`, scored token-overlap recall of PRIOR WORKSTATION INTERACTIONS in this installation, tenant-scoped, prefixed with its own "[native memory recall ... PROVENANCE LIMIT ...]" banner. It consults no science, technology, business, operations or law source, and no such source exists in the system to consult (Owner ruling 2026-09-29: no egress). So "the gateway is called with augment=False, SO §4.3's five disciplines are composed from the request's own term counts" is a false causal claim, and I proved it false by flipping the flag: augment=True produces the same term counts. The term counts come from the deterministic native floor serving because no model is available (AI_DISABLE_LOCAL=1) — the environment, which the harness rule excludes. Worse for the finding, augment=False here is a deliberate correctness FIX, not an omission: the in-code history (W332, W488, W489 — 29 callers) records that inherited recall prepended another request's content and presented it as analysis of the caller's own subject, and my augment=True run re-exhibited exactly that subject-capture. Acting on this finding by enabling augmentation for the journey would reintroduce a known tier-1-class defect. On tier: the reached surface discloses at three layers in the SAME render block ({result && ( at line 618 contains both cards). Above the stages, the provenance card prints "native ×13 · no external" and, in amber, "The deterministic native floor served 13 of these calls — honest structured composition from your inputs, not generative model reasoning. Run a local model (see /native-ai) for generative output", plus stages_note from my own run: "5 of 5 stages were served by the deterministic floor and are not assessable — the verification proxies cannot fail on floor output, so no verdict is claimed for them". Inside the §4.3 card, every one of the five sections — including "Best & Latest Approaches" and "Innovative Options", the two carrying the discipline claim — states "The list below is the request's most frequent terms, not findings about them." No figure is fabricated and nothing is certified: stages_verified reads 0/0. The only residue is that the card's static 10px subtitle still reads "Best & latest approaches + innovative options across science · technology · business · operations · law" when, by design (no egress), no external source is consulted even with a model running — a label-versus-capability disclosure nicety, tier 3, not the assessed tier-2 PARTIAL whose stated mechanism I refuted.
- **refuter's evidence:** I ran my OWN journey against the live backend: POST http://127.0.0.1:8086/api/v1/genesis/journey with problem "Sheffield community dental clinics turn away adult patients because NHS contract units of dental activity run out by February each year" (domain/realm healthcare/enterprise), 200, 40,236 bytes. stage_3_innovate_research reproduced the shape the assessor described: five sections (SOCH, AQAL, HOSHIYARI, Best & Latest Approaches, Innovative Options), each a five-item list of my request's own bigrams ("sheffield community", "community dental", "dental activity"). ai_provenance = {"served_by":{"native":13},"any_external":false,"body_served_by":{"native":5}}. I then tested the finding's MECHANISM directly against the gateway, in-process, with the same stage-3 prompt and augment=False vs augment=True: BOTH returned served_by=native and the identical term-count frame — and augment=True was strictly WORSE, because the recalled text made the engine's own instruction banner the subject ("Native structured frame for 'Best & Latest Approaches' over: You are the IDBO Innovate & Research engine..." with terms "innovate research", "research engine"). I read C:\Users\rehan\Workstation\agentic_core\ai\gateway.py:199-230 (_augment) and :288, C:\Users\rehan\Workstation\agentic_core\api\genesis.py:271-281 and :348-358, and C:\Users\rehan\Workstation\apps\workstation-superapp\src\pages\synthesis\GenesisJourney.tsx:618, 634-660, 706-715.

### R2.8 · DELIVERED — §13's three integrated surfaces are genuinely real and carry an honest "content pending the owned model" state into the shipped HTML — **DELIVERED**

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** DELIVERED entries carry no tier.

No compiled binary is claimed anywhere.

GET /repo/ship → "shipped":true, surfaces repo/website/webapp/mobile/board_pack, "version_control":{"mechanism":"git","commit":"6d78a73f4d70","commits_total":4}, and BUSINESS_PLAN.md ships the pending sentence rather than invented prose.
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). Reproduced as stated, with my own independently chosen enterprise rather than the assessor's. (1) website: three cross-linked static HTML pages plus a stylesheet, real nav with an `active` class, all four served 200 through /website/page/* (the stylesheet is served under the name "styles", so my first probe for "styles.css" 404ing was my URL, not a defect). (2) webapp/app.js is 2596 bytes of genuine client-side vanilla JS - fetch('data.json'), four tabs, a live resource filter that restores the caret, HTML escaping - and data.json is 3436 bytes of this entity's real data, not placeholders. (3) mobile is a genuine installable PWA: manifest.webmanifest with display:standalone and a maskable icon, icon.svg, and sw.js with install/activate/fetch handlers and const CACHE = 'vsb-pwa-v1'. No compiled binary is claimed anywhere; the only hits for binary vocabulary are disclaimers ("NOT a compiled native iOS/Android app, not deployed/hosted", "not a compiled app"). The §15.6 corollary holds in the SHIPPED BYTES, not merely in the API: web/index.html's hero prints "Its approach: content pending the owned model - this enterprise has not yet composed its own concept", about.html repeats it per section, webapp/data.json carries it in concept / business_plan.executive_summary / organisation.ceo, and BUSINESS_PLAN.md ships four pending sections plus the founder's own problem statement as Vision - no invented prose and no fabricated figure anywhere. The journey's own established_vsb reports body_pending {concept, design, commercialisation, operations} all true with status "body pending" and status_basis naming the four sections, so the top-level surface agrees with the files. Two side observations, neither tier 1 and neither overturning the finding: agentic_core\api\vsb.py:470 emits the footer "generated in-house on Workstation's own AI fabric" as an unconditional f-string rather than computing it from served_by (which was "template" under AI_DISABLE_LOCAL=1) - it survives only because the same page's body states the content is pending the owned model, so at most tier 3 and the "a basis string is code" shape; and GET /repo reports file_count 13 with integrated_surfaces "web/index.html (scaffold)" while 29 files exist, and manifest.json calls the surfaces "scaffolds for later increments (NOT built/compiled/running apps)" - a stale repo-stage UNDER-claim, the opposite of a tier-1 overclaim. DELIVERED stands; no tier applies.
- **refuter's evidence:** Ran my OWN journey, not the assessor's: POST /api/v1/genesis/journey {problem: "Smallholder date farmers in Oman lose a third of each harvest to spoilage before it reaches a buyer", domain: agriculture, name: "Nakhla Cold Chain Trust", establish: true, ship_output: true} -> 200 in 50s, vsb-d42be93723, initial_ship.shipped=true, surfaces [board_pack, mobile, repo, webapp, website], commit 65dbf1121af0. Then GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-d42be93723/repo/ship -> shipped:true, coherent_whole:true, surfaces_refused:{}, version_control {mechanism:git, commit:65dbf1121af0, commits_total:4}; `git log` in the generated repo shows the four real commits repo:/website:/webapp:/mobile:. Then EXECUTED every serve route: /website/page/{index,about,solution,styles} -> 200 (1337/1239/1098/1062 B), /webapp/page/{index,app.js,data.json,styles.css} -> 200 (app.js 2568 B), /mobile/page/{index,app.js,sw.js,manifest.webmanifest,icon.svg,data.json,styles.css} -> 200 with correct content types (application/manifest+json, image/svg+xml, text/javascript). ### R2.9 · DELIVERED — a regenerated deliverable reports its own REGRESSION on the surface, in red, with the delta — **DELIVERED**

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** DELIVERED entries carry no tier.
- **claim:** §13 — deliverables are "continually-developing, learning ... each able to keep researching, improving"
- **observed:** I executed POST /api/v1/deliverables/deliv-a206edb8/regenerate and read the response, then checked the renderer at apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/Deliverables.tsx:241-247, which chips `v{n} {verdict} {delta}%` in `bg-vital/15 text-vital` when the verdict is 'regressed' and preserves the prior version for reading (lines 277-290).
- **evidence:** regenerate response: "improvement":{"compared_against_prior_draft":true,"coverage_before":1.0,"coverage_after":0.833,"coverage_delta":-0.167,"sections_uncovered_after":["Core Components","Technology & Delivery Plan","MVP Scope","Operational Intelligence"],"verdict":"regressed","note":"This version scored LOWER than the one it replaced. The previous version is preserved in history."} — rendered as "v2 regressed -17%".
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). Reproduced as stated, on my own input rather than the assessor's. The regression is MEASURED (from the gate's delivery_coverage, not asserted), it is SIGNED (negative delta produces the verdict and the note), it names the specific sections that went uncovered, it reaches the surface in red with the delta, and the superseded version remains readable — so a user is told the regeneration made the deliverable worse rather than silently receiving a worse draft. Two honest qualifications that do not change the verdict: (1) the coverage instrument is a case-insensitive substring search over the whole document, so a heading's words appearing anywhere in prose counts as coverage — I tripped this myself and had to assert the words absent; the chip's tooltip does describe it as coverage, and quality.py:267 documents the instrument, so it is disclosed, not over-claimed. (2) Under AI_DISABLE_LOCAL=1 the native deterministic floor emits every declared heading, so a model-free regeneration can essentially only come back "unchanged" — the improvement/regression machinery is real but, in this environment, is exercised mainly by reconfigured or verbatim-ingested content; compared_against_prior_draft is computed from the prompt actually sent (deliverables.py:139 reports it from `prior`, not from caller intent), and the detail page shows the provenance badge, so the surface does not claim a model improved anything when none ran. Neither qualification is a reached surface saying something untrue, so there is nothing here to raise to a tier-1 claim; the DELIVERED verdict stands unchanged.
- **refuter's evidence:** I did not reuse the assessor's deliverable. I created my own (POST /api/v1/deliverables/produce -> deliv-7e0ad93d, 6 declared sections, coverage 1.0), then drove it through two of my own regenerations. First I regenerated deliv-3ccb0e72 and deliv-7a093412 with my own briefs/section sets (both came back verdict "unchanged", delta 0.0 — the deterministic native floor emits every declared heading, so coverage stays 1.0). To make the regression path actually fire I posted a deliberately thinner refined draft to POST /api/v1/deliverables/deliv-7e0ad93d/regenerate that omits two declared headings (asserting in my script that the strings "sensor"/"costing" appear nowhere in it — my first try silently kept coverage at 1.0 because I had mentioned both words in prose, which is the substring nature of the instrument). Result: {"coverage_before":1.0,"coverage_after":0.667,"coverage_delta":-0.333,"sections_uncovered_after":["Sensor Net","Costing"],"verdict":"regressed","note":"This version scored LOWER than the one it replaced. The previous version is preserved in history."}. GET /api/v1/deliverables/deliv-7e0ad93d then carries that same improvement block, quality_assurance.quality is present (so the renderer's gate is open), 3 versions are stored and v2's 413-char content is still readable alongside the 372-char live content. I read the computation at C:\Users\rehan\Workstation\agentic_core\api\deliverables.py:1065-1106 (delta from the QMS gate's own delivery_coverage, verdict from the sign of the delta, note only when negative) and C:\Users\rehan\Workstation\agentic_core\vbs\quality.py:267-280 (coverage = fraction of declared sections present; None when none declared), and the renderer at C:\Users\rehan\Workstation\apps\workstation-superapp\src\pages\Deliverables.tsx:237-290, where verdict 'regressed' chips `v{n} regressed {delta}%` in bg-vital/15 text-vital, adds a red plain-language line "This version scored lower than the one it replaced - the previous version is kept in history.", and renders per-version buttons for reading the prior draft. For my record the chip reads "v3 regressed -33%".

## R3 — §5 + §17.3 + §17.4 — the living organisation, the living business system layers, the three integration modes

### R3.0 · The living plan attributes the platform's own placeholder sentence to the Owner, and that attribution is what keeps the heading "Chief's Opening" — **DOC_OVERCLAIM** · tier 1

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** A reached surface tells the founder that text they never wrote is their own words, and the same miss defeats the W505/FU-158 conditional heading so the block is still headed "Chief's Opening" over a sentence that says nothing has been composed. Two untrue statements about authorship, on the page, about the artefact §5 makes the Chief's.
- **claim:** §5: "The Chief owns the living Business Plan — and it now opens, as a Chief-owned artefact, with an **Executive Summary · Concept · Vision** (the founder's idea and intent framed by the Chief)"
- **observed:** Executed GET http://127.0.0.1:8086/api/v1/business-plan?scope=vsb-078e6b3af2 (the one established VSB, origin genesis_journey). The `concept` field holds the platform's floor placeholder, yet provenance.field_sources.concept is "owner_supplied" and provenance.opening_written_by asserts the concept is the Owner's words. Source: agentic_core/api/genesis.py:1003 builds `_templated` and appends "concept" ONLY when req.concept is blank — a non-blank floor placeholder passed through establish therefore counts as the Owner's; genesis.py:1010 stamps it "owner_supplied" and genesis.py:1015 emits the sentence. Reader: apps/workstation-superapp/src/components/PlanOpening.tsx:142-145 prints opening_written_by; :157-166 gives a "template" badge only to fields in templated_fields, so concept renders with NO source badge; :79-85 computes allTemplated and, because concept is non-empty and untemplated, the heading is "Chief’s Opening" instead of "Plan opening — generated from your establish answers". Rendered on the Business Plan page and the VSB Cockpit plan tab (both import PlanOpening).
- **evidence:** API: "concept": "content pending the owned model — this enterprise has not yet composed its own concept" with "field_sources": {"concept": "owner_supplied", …} and "opening_written_by": "code templates filled from the establish request — not the Chief, and not a model. The concept is the Owner's own words. Edit any field to replace it with your own; a model or the Chief has composed nothing here."  Code: genesis.py:1015 `+ (f"The concept is the Owner's own words. " if "concept" not in _templated else "")`
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). Reproduced exactly as stated, on my own independently chosen VSB, and my own input makes the claim false in a stronger way than the assessor's did. I supplied real founder prose as the concept. The platform DISCARDED it (floor-served, so _resolve_body_fields replaced it with the platform's own placeholder) and then labelled that placeholder "the Owner's own words." So the surface is not merely attributing unwritten text to the founder — it deleted the founder's actual sentence and attributed its own substitute to them. Two untrue statements reach the page about the one artefact §5 makes the Chief's: "The concept is the Owner's own words", and the heading "Chief’s Opening" sitting over a sentence that says nothing has been composed. The heading failure is the same miss: the W505/FU-158 conditional heading is defeated solely because concept is non-empty-and-untemplated, which is precisely the state the placeholder creates.  I checked hard for a disclosure that would pull the tier down, because the rule is that a disclosed shortfall is tier 2 or 3. The page does print two true things about the same field: PlanOpening.tsx:128-133 renders "pending the owned model: concept" from provenance.body_pending (which my record does contain), and the concept text itself says it is pending. But neither discloses THIS defect. They contradict the authorship claim rather than qualifying it; the page ends up asserting both "nothing was written here" and "these are the Owner's own words" about one field, and the false one is the specific, affirmative claim about authorship. A surface that prints an untrue statement beside a true one has still printed the untrue one, and a founder has no way to tell which to believe. This is also not the AI_DISABLE_LOCAL environment showing through: the environment explains WHY the concept is a placeholder, and the placeholder says so honestly — the defect is the authorship stamp the code puts on it, which would be just as false with a model running, because any floor-served field takes the same path.  One thing the assessor did not have, which hardens the finding: the guard meant to cover exactly this is blind. integration_tests\test_mvp_spine.py:25218 calls gn._seed_plan_from_journey directly with a hand-built SimpleNamespace, so it never runs _resolve_body_fields. It asserts owner_said["concept"] == "owner_supplied" for a concept it hands in non-blank, and that assertion is TRUE of the defect — the guard cannot distinguish a concept the Owner actually wrote from the platform's placeholder, because it never produces the overwrite that creates the condition. It is green now. That is the "a guard must drive the state" class: the guard drives the seeder, not the pipeline that feeds it.  Tier 1 stands and cannot be corrected upward, so the direction is SAME on both verdict and tier. The fix is at the seam, not in the wording: the pendency _resolve_body_fields already returns (and stores as body_pending) has to reach _seed_plan_from_journey so a floor-replaced field is recorded as neither owner_supplied nor an establish_template but as pending, and the guard has to drive the real establish path rather than the seeder alone.
- **refuter's evidence:** I did not repeat the assessor's record. I established MY OWN VSB through the live backend — POST http://127.0.0.1:8086/api/v1/genesis/establish, owner_id "refuter_r30", name "Degla Dry-Store Waqf (R3.0 refuter)", a Tunisian date-farming problem, and a concept I wrote myself ("## Concept\nA shared solar dehumidified dry-store co-located at the souk, with a weigh-in ledger so each farmer's lot is tracked by moisture class and engines assure the grading."), with ai_provenance.served_by_agent marking the body agents "native". It returned vsb-ec3c9b0809 with body_pending.concept = true. GET /api/v1/business-plan?scope=vsb-ec3c9b0809 then returned concept = "content pending the owned model — this enterprise has not yet composed its own concept", field_sources.concept = "owner_supplied", templated_fields = [executive_summary, vision, mission, strategy] (concept absent), and opening_written_by = "code templates filled from the establish request — not the Chief, and not a model. The concept is the Owner's own words. Edit any field to replace it with your own; a model or the Chief has composed nothing here." I also confirmed the assessor's vsb-078e6b3af2 returns the same shape. I then read the mechanism and the reader: C:\Users\rehan\Workstation\agentic_core\api\genesis.py line 1091 calls _resolve_body_fields(req) BEFORE line 1195 calls _seed_plan_from_journey(req) (and the SSE path repeats the order at 1305 before 1358), so by the time line 1004 tests `if not (req.concept or "").strip()` the field has already been overwritten at line 972 with _PENDING_BODY (defined line 880); non-blank, so concept never enters _templated, line 1010 stamps it "owner_supplied" and line 1015 appends the Owner's-own-words sentence. In C:\Users\rehan\Workstation\apps\workstation-superapp\src\components\PlanOpening.tsx I read :79-82 (allTemplated false because concept is non-empty and untemplated, so heading = "Chief’s Opening"), :141-146 (opening_written_by printed verbatim whenever templated.size > 0, and it is 4), and :157-166 (concept gets no source badge at all, because that branch requires sources[k] !== 'owner_supplied'). Both readers confirmed: BusinessPlan.tsx:175 and VSBCockpit.tsx:702 render PlanOpening with the default fields, and ABOVE_FOLD (:56) includes 'concept'.

### R3.1 · The Board Pack reports all four §17.3 layers PRESENT when Strategic holds only a pending placeholder and Action Plan holds only a roster — and the honest basis for a present layer cannot be reached on the page — **PARTIAL** · tier 1

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** The chip's own tooltip promises presence means the layer holds something, and it reports four of four over a placeholder sentence and a roster. The honest basis strings exist but render only for layers the chip already excluded, so no reader can reach them. This is the W496/FU-104 class re-committed inside its own fix.
- **claim:** §17.3: "**Strategic** (AI CEO; quarterly + market signal): position, growth, resource posture. · **Action Plan** (BTO; weekly + KPI-triggered): sprint priorities, corrective actions, timelines. … **Board Pack** (on-demand): assembled fresh from live data"
- **observed:** Executed POST http://127.0.0.1:8086/api/v1/vsb/vsb-078e6b3af2/board-pack (HTTP 200). layers_present lists all four. agentic_core/api/vsb.py:1260-1263 sets `present: bool(_ceo_spec)` — truthiness of a field, so the string "content pending the owned model …" counts as a composed strategic layer and the basis then asserts "the AI CEO specification recorded on this entity". vsb.py:1272-1273 computes layers_present from that flag. Reader: apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/synthesis/GenesisJourney.tsx:1131-1136 chips layers_present with the title "a layer counts as present only if it holds something; the pack says which"; :1137-1144 renders the "N empty" chip — whose tooltip is the ONLY place a layer's basis appears — solely for layers NOT in layers_present. With 0 empty, that chip and every present layer's basis are unreachable, including action_plan's own admission.
- **evidence:** "layers_present": ["action_plan","constitutional","operational","strategic"]; "strategic": {"ceo": "content pending the owned model — this enterprise has not yet composed its own commercialisation", "present": true, "basis": "the AI CEO specification recorded on this entity"}; "action_plan": {… "present": true, "basis": "the entity's standing board roster - a roster, not a plan of action; no action items are recorded here"}; top-level "note": "Living Business System — on-demand Board Pack, assembled fresh from live data, DCS-registered."
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). REPRODUCED with my own independently chosen entity, and the tier is if anything better supported than the assessor argued.  The reached surface says something untrue. The chip at GenesisJourney.tsx:1131-1136 renders "action_plan . constitutional . operational . strategic" under the heading "Board Pack" with the tooltip "a layer counts as present only if it holds something; the pack says which". On my VSB the strategic layer holds a sentence whose content is the statement that no content was composed. The chip asserts the opposite of what the layer holds.  The honest basis cannot be reached. Confirmed by exhaustive grep plus reading the whole card: a layer's basis renders only inside the pack-layers-empty tooltip, which is gated on at least one layer being absent. At 4/4 that chip does not render at all. And my control shows that even when it DOES render it lists only the excluded layers, so action_plan's self-admission ("a roster, not a plan of action; no action items are recorded here") is unreachable in every state. The rendered narrative even promises "the strategic and action layers are present only if this entity carries a CEO specification and a board (each says which below)" - the page does not keep that promise.  This is NOT the AI_DISABLE_LOCAL environment excuse, for two reasons I verified myself and the assessor did not cite. First, the repo's own pending predicate is in the SAME FILE at vsb.py:249 - `any("content pending the owned model" in b for b in body)` returns "template" precisely so a gate records "not assessable" instead of passing scaffold. The board pack had the correct test available 1000 lines up and used bare truthiness instead. Second, the entity itself carries body_pending {"ceo_specification": true}, written at vsb.py:1899 by `ceo_spec.startswith("content pending the owned model")`. The system already knows the layer is a placeholder; the pack surface fails to SAY so - which the brief names as exactly what keeps a pending state at tier 1.  Verdict stays PARTIAL rather than STUB: the assembly machinery is real - constitutional is genuinely derived and operational is a live snapshot, the content hash, version/unchanged-since, DCS seal, QA coverage and the W485 entity-vs-pack §11 split all compute from state. Two of the four §17.3 layers are a placeholder and a roster, and the presence report over them is false. Real machinery with a false claim on top is PARTIAL.  Tier stays 1 and does not come down to 2 or 3: a tier-down requires the surface to disclose the shortfall, and here the only honest strings are structurally gated out of the render for exactly the layers that need them. The guard that was supposed to hold this closed (test_mvp_spine.py:21704-21707) asserts only that the literals "layers_present", "pack-layers-present" and "pack-layers-empty" appear in the source files - the presence-check-cannot-see-a-disabled-gate class - so it stays green through the defect. The assessor's reading of this as the W496/FU-104 class re-committed inside its own fix is correct.
- **refuter's evidence:** Chose my OWN VSB rather than the assessor's vsb-078e6b3af2: surveyed all 10 entities via GET /api/v1/vsb and GET /api/v1/vsb/{id}, then executed POST http://127.0.0.1:8086/api/v1/vsb/vsb-f2eb9f5037/board-pack (Khalas Cold Chain Cooperative, HTTP 200) -> layers_present ["action_plan","constitutional","operational","strategic"], layers.strategic.ceo = "content pending the owned model - this enterprise has not yet composed its own CEO specification", present true, basis "the AI CEO specification recorded on this entity". Also ran my own CONTROL, POST .../vsb-69df699437/board-pack (Palm Biochar Probe, ceo_specification == ""), which returned 3 present + strategic basis "EMPTY - no CEO specification was composed...", proving bool() only fails on the placeholder and that action_plan's "a roster, not a plan of action" admission is unreachable in BOTH the 4/4 and 3/4 states. Read agentic_core/api/vsb.py:1255-1275 (the bool(_ceo_spec) flag and layers_present), the full pack card apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/synthesis/GenesisJourney.tsx:1119-1186, and grepped the repo: layers[*].basis renders at exactly ONE place, GenesisJourney.tsx:1140, inside the pack-layers-empty tooltip gated on layers_present.length < Object.keys(layers).length.
