### R5.9 · Care's clinical risk tool computes a real NEWS2 score from the published table — not an AI guess — and says which is which — **DELIVERED**

- **claim:** §3A offering 1 — domain tools that let a user "**assess**" in their domain on Workstation's own native AI; §15.6 honesty over polish; §15.5 "**verified · tested · validated**"
- **observed:** Executed `POST /api/v1/care/risk-assess` with a full NEWS2 observation set (RR 22, SpO2 94 on air, SBP 110, pulse 105, alert, 38.2 °C) and checked the arithmetic against the RCP 2017 table by hand: RR 21-24 = 2, SpO2 94-95 = 1, air = 0, SBP 101-110 = 1, pulse 91-110 = 1, alert = 0, temp 38.1-39.0 = 1 → total 6, and 5-6 is the medium band with the urgent-response threshold. The response returns exactly that, per-component, with the table named — a deterministic computation, not a model output — and the narrative beside it is separately labelled as the floor's structured frame. The disclaimer separates the two halves explicitly.
- **evidence:** Live: `"score":{"table":"NEWS2 — Royal College of Physicians, 2017","spo2_scale":1,"components":{"respiratory_rate":{"value":22.0,"points":2},"spo2_scale_1":{"value":94.0,"points":1},"air_or_oxygen":{"value":"air","points":0},"systolic_bp":{"value":110.0,"points":1},"pulse":{"value":105.0,"points":1},"consciousness":{"value":"alert","points":0},"temperature":{"value":38.2,"points":1}},"total":6,"band":"medium"}` and `"disclaimer":"The score is computed in-house from the published table (NEWS2 RCP 2017 · MUST BAPEN · Waterlow · NICE CG161 factor count) and is a decision aid, not a diagnosis; the narrative is an AI interpretation aid only. Clinical judgement by a qualified professional is required."`
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). The finding reproduces exactly, with my own independently chosen inputs, so it stands as DELIVERED and the direction is SAME. There is nothing to correct up or down.  The substance is real machinery, not a narrated claim. compute_score() is pure deterministic arithmetic over the published lookup tables; it is called at care.py:119 BEFORE any text generation, its summary is injected into the prompt as authoritative with an explicit "do NOT recompute or restate a different total", and the score block is returned as its own key. My six executions include two cases the assessor did not try - a deliberately incomplete set and a scale-2 set - and both behaved more conservatively than a DELIVERED claim requires: the incomplete one returned a total of 6 with band:null and the words "the total is a lower bound", rather than banding a partial observation set, and the scale-2 one applied the correct air/oxygen split for SpO2 >= 93 instead of guessing.  Three things that could have made this a tier-1 claim, all of which the surface handles: - A tool with no table could have produced a fabricated number. It does not: available:false with a note naming the absence, and CareHub renders that note in amber instead of a score. - NICE CG161 has no validated total. The response calls it a factor COUNT in the table string, the note and the response line, and the page prints "factor count" rather than a score. - The deterministic floor narrative could have worn a green badge or a QMS pass. It does not: ai_provenance.served_by is "native", qms_gate_passed is null, and qmsChip maps null to a grey "QMS -" chip whose title is the live basis string "not assessable - floor-served: the deterministic floor emits the requested headings, so coverage cannot fail by construction ... no instrument here can say whether this content is good". The narrative itself opens "_[Workstation native structured engine - owned, no external dependency]_" and labels its own bullet list "the request's most frequent terms, not findings about them".  Two honest observations, neither a defect against this finding and neither tier-1, recorded so they are not mistaken for a clean sweep: (a) the route-level disclaimer calls the narrative "an AI interpretation aid only", which under AI_DISABLE_LOCAL=1 is a deterministic floor rather than a model - but that is the environment, and the provenance block, the QMS chip title and the narrative's own first line all say floor/native, so it is disclosed; (b) the md/txt/html export carries a provenance header and the narrative text but not the computed score block, so a downloaded file omits the score - it does not misstate it (the AI is instructed not to restate a total), so at most a tier-3 completeness nit on the export, outside this finding's scope.  No tier is applicable: this is a DELIVERED finding, so there is no reached surface telling a user something untrue, no fabricated figure, and no certification of what could not be assessed.
- **refuter's evidence:** Executed POST http://127.0.0.1:8086/api/v1/care/risk-assess live, six times, with MY OWN inputs (not the assessor's RR22/SpO2-94 case), and hand-checked each against the RCP 2017 NEWS2 table:  (1) High-acuity case I chose: RR 9 /min, SpO2 91%, on O2, SBP 85, pulse 135, "new confusion", temp 34.8 -> my hand arithmetic 1+3+2+3+3+3+3 = 18, high band, emergency response. Returned exactly: components {respiratory_rate 1, spo2_scale_1 3, air_or_oxygen 2, systolic_bp 3, pulse 3, consciousness 3, temperature 3}, "total":18, "band":"high", "single_parameter_3":true, "complete":true. Note the string parsing worked ("9 /min", "91%", "O2").  (2) All-normal case: RR 13, SpO2 97, air, SBP 120, pulse 52, alert, 36.5 -> total 0, band low, "routine monitoring - minimum 12 hourly". Correct (pulse 51-90 = 0, not the 41-50 = 1 row).  (3) Scale-2 case with a Fahrenheit temperature: RR 22, SpO2 88, spo2_scale 2, air, SBP 140, pulse 70, alert, "99.5 F" -> spo2_scale_2 scored 0 (88-92 is the prescribed-target band on air), temp converted to 37.5 C = 0, total 2, low, 4-6 hourly, and the warning "temperature given in degF - converted to 37.5 degC" is surfaced. Correct.  (4) Deliberately INCOMPLETE case: RR 26, SpO2 90, air only -> total 6 but "band":null, response "a single parameter already scores 3 - the low-medium urgent ward-based response applies now; the total is a lower bound until the missing observations are recorded", missing [systolic_bp, pulse, consciousness (ACVPU), temperature]. It refuses to band a lower bound - that is the opposite of an overclaim.  (5) falls_risk with my own data (falls_history yes, age 82, home_hazards no) -> table "NICE CG161 multifactorial falls risk - factor COUNT, not a validated score", total 2, band "multifactorial assessment warranted", response "...this count is not a score".  (6) A tool with NO published table (dementia_care) -> "available":false, "no published arithmetic exists for this tool - the narrative below is an AI aid only, not a score". No fabricated number.  Read the code: C:\Users\rehan\Workstation\agentic_core\care\scoring.py (all five NEWS2 cut-point ladders verified line by line against RCP 2017 - RR, SpO2 scale 1 and scale 2 air/oxygen split, SBP, pulse, ACVPU, temp - all correct, as are the 0 / 1-4 / single-3 / 5-6 / >=7 band rules and their ordering); C:\Users\rehan\Workstation\agentic_core\api\care.py lines 106-161 (score computed BEFORE the prompt, summary injected as "authoritative - do NOT recompute"); C:\Users\rehan\Workstation\apps\workstation-superapp\src\pages\domains\CareHub.tsx lines 71-120 (the score block, data-testid="care-score"); C:\Users\rehan\Workstation\apps\workstation-superapp\src\components\DomainTool.tsx line 317 (renderExtra renders ABOVE the narrative <pre>, disclaimer below it) and lines 256-267 of src\lib\api.ts (qmsChip).

## R6 — §8 + §12 + §17.5 — the biomimetic living organism, the economic organism, the ten architecture invariants

### R6.0 · The Cognition page prints API-surface coverage as "realisation 97%" — the naming invariant, broken on a reached surface by a consumer that drops the producer's own caveat — **DOC_OVERCLAIM** · tier 1

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** A reached page tells the user the vision is 97% realised. The figure is routers-mounted-and-stores-non-empty, which the producer states and this consumer drops. One writer was fixed (line 261, W496/FU-111) and the sibling card on the same page was not — the figure travels without its basis into the one place a reader looks for "how far along is this".
- **claim:** §8 (W482): "A quantity may carry the name of a law only when computed by that law (the naming invariant): API-surface coverage is not vision realisation; payload size over a thousand is not bits erased; a SHA is not a Halo2 proof."
- **observed:** Executed GET /api/v1/transformation/realisation → overall 0.97 WITH measure "API surface coverage — pillar routers mounted and stores non-empty; not delivery". Executed GET /api/v1/cognition/knowledge → code_live_state = {"api": "/api/v1/plan/state + /api/v1/transformation/realisation", "overall_realisation": 0.97} and NO measure field (agentic_core/api/cognition.py:90 copies only the number). apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/CognitionIntegration.tsx:194 renders that number as `realisation ${...}%` in the "Code / Live-State" knowledge-layer card, with no qualifier. The producer's checks are route-mount and non-empty-store lambdas (agentic_core/api/transformation.py:82-130).
- **evidence:** CognitionIntegration.tsx:194 — `{ k: 'Code / Live-State', d: `realisation ${knowledge.code_live_state?.overall_realisation != null ? Math.round(knowledge.code_live_state.overall_realisation * 100) + '%' : '—'}` }` ... while line 261 of the SAME page renders the same figure correctly: "API surface coverage {…}% (routers mounted and stores non-empty — not delivery)".
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). STANDS and the tier is confirmed at 1, but the assessor was too kind on three counts.  REPRODUCED CORE: cognition.py:88-91 copies the bare number and drops the producer's `measure`; CognitionIntegration.tsx:194 renders it in the always-mounted "Four Synchronised Knowledge Layers" card as `realisation {n}%` with no qualifier and no title attribute. The card is gated only on `{knowledge && ...}`, and `knowledge` is fetched in the mount useEffect (line 33) — so it renders unconditionally for any visitor to /cognition. The figure is route-mount-and-non-empty-store, which the producer itself states and this consumer does not.  HARSHER 1 — the number is worse than reported. On my own live instance overall_realisation is 1.0, not 0.97. The reached card therefore prints "realisation 100%": the page tells the user the IDBO vision is fully realised, on a figure that means eleven pillar prefixes are mounted and four stores are non-empty.  HARSHER 2 — the mitigation the assessor cited does not exist in this state. They wrote that "line 261 of the SAME page renders the same figure correctly". It cannot: line 251-252 branches on `align.gaps_routed.length === 0`, and the live align response returns gaps_routed = 0 (necessarily, since realisation is 1.0 — no pillar is short, so no gap is routed). The page takes the zero branch and line 261 never renders. The correctly-labelled writer is unreachable precisely when the figure is at its most misleading, so the unqualified "realisation 100%" is the ONLY place this figure appears on the page. The honest writer is conditional on a user POST; the broken one is on by default.  HARSHER 3 — a second untrue sentence on the same reached surface, unreported. The branch that actually renders prints, in emerald, "Fully aligned — no open vision gaps." That is the naming invariant broken again by the same figure: zero routed gaps means every pillar's route-mount and non-empty-store lambdas passed, not that the vision has no open gaps. The repo's own delivery register contradicts it (P2 is open). The assessor found one broken consumer; the live state has two statements on one page, and the writer they credited as correct is dead code.  NOT THE ENVIRONMENT: nothing here depends on AI_DISABLE_LOCAL=1 or on a model running. The producer computes honestly and discloses; the defect is purely a consumer dropping the caveat, which is the "fix every writer, not one" / "a badge in the DOM is not a label on the text" class — provenance must travel with the figure. TransformationDashboard.tsx, the third reader, does it right in both places (header prose plus the label beside the 5xl number at line 217), proving the caveat was known and simply not carried to this card. No test in the repo guards `code_live_state`, the card wording, or the "Fully aligned" string, which is why the W496/FU-111 fix at line 261 left the sibling untouched and nothing went red.  Tier stays 1: a reached page states something untrue to the one reader asking "how far along is this".
- **refuter's evidence:** Executed all three routes against the live backend myself (not the assessor's numbers): (1) GET /api/v1/transformation/realisation → overall_realisation = **1.0** with measure "API surface coverage — pillar routers mounted and stores non-empty; not delivery (the delivery measure is /api/v1/plan/state)", evidence_counts {vsbs:14, economy_ledgers:9, resources:41, compositions:3}. (2) GET /api/v1/cognition/knowledge → code_live_state = {"api": "...", "overall_realisation": 1.0} and NO measure field; confirmed agentic_core/api/cognition.py:88-91 copies only `.get("overall_realisation")`. (3) POST /api/v1/cognition/align {"execute":false} with my own body → overall_realisation 1.0, measure present, **gaps_routed count = 0**. Read the producer's checks (agentic_core/api/transformation.py:80-130 — route-mount `_has()` and non-empty-store lambdas). ### R6.1 · The shell-wide "Cardiovascular" vital is host CPU idleness, and the basis the API computes for it is rendered by nothing — **PARTIAL** *(assessed API_ONLY)* · tier 2 *(assessed tier 1)*

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** The organism's circulation layer shows green exactly when the host machine is idle, on the one widget present on every screen. The honest sentence exists and stops at the API: provenance must travel with the output, and here it does not reach the render.
- **claim:** §8: "The 7 biomimetic layers (Genome · Nervous · Immune · Cardiovascular · Respiratory · Musculoskeletal · Endocrine) … make it dynamic, adaptive, responsive"; §17.2 realisation map: "the 'cardiovascular' biometric is CPU-idle relabelled".
- **observed:** agentic_core/app_mvp.py:523 computes `resource_flow = max(0.0, 100.0 - cpu)` and app_mvp.py:599-601 serves it as "cardiovascular" WITH `resource_flow_basis: "host CPU headroom (100 − cpu%) — spare capacity, not workload"` and `host_cpu_percent`. The hook types both (apps/workstation-superapp/src/hooks/useWorkstationBiometrics.ts:23-24) and a repo-wide grep for `resource_flow_basis|host_cpu_percent` across apps/workstation-superapp/src/**/*.tsx returns NOTHING — no component renders either. What a user gets is a pulsing dot whose only label is title="Cardiovascular" (BiometricStatus.tsx:167 and :192), green above 80 (cardioClass, BiometricStatus.tsx:60-66), mounted on every page via Shell.tsx:296.
- **evidence:** BiometricStatus.tsx:8-10 (the component's own comment): "the reading is HOST CPU HEADROOM (100 − cpu%), so high means the host is idle … It says nothing about whether the platform is doing any work." The tooltip the user actually sees says only "Cardiovascular".
- **refutation: STOOD, verdict CORRECTED API_ONLY → PARTIAL.** The mechanism reproduces exactly as described: the shell-wide "Cardiovascular" dot is coloured from 100 - host CPU%, its only label is title="Cardiovascular" (BiometricStatus.tsx:167, :192), and it is mounted on every page via Shell.tsx:296. The honest sentence the API computes does not reach THAT widget. That much stands.  But the finding's title and its API_ONLY verdict are wrong on the facts. The basis is NOT "rendered by nothing": Introspection.tsx:107 prints the same figure with the label "Host CPU headroom" - the basis's substance, on a registered route. The assessor grepped the two field NAMES (resource_flow_basis|host_cpu_percent) instead of the value they qualify, so they missed the one reader that labels it correctly. That is this repo's own "grep the call, not the method name" trap, and it is why API_ONLY overstates the case.  Tier 1 fails on all three of its own triggers, which I checked individually against the reached surface. The shell dot fabricates no figure (it displays no number). It states nothing untrue: its only words are "Cardiovascular", and the adjacent state label is derived from workload.platform_busy, not from resource_flow - the W489 fix - so the inversion the assessor's vision clause worries about is already closed. Live right now the widget reads red dot + "Idle" on a 95%-CPU host, which is not a false claim about the platform. And it certifies nothing it could not assess: with the backend unreachable cardioClass(flow, live=false) returns bg-slate-600 grey rather than fabricated green health, and the hook's DEFAULT carries live:false for exactly that reason.  What genuinely remains is an unexplained colour encoding - the tooltip names the biological layer but never the quantity, so the ubiquitous widget carries no provenance. That is a real disclosure shortfall worth fixing (the tooltip should say "host CPU headroom"), but it is a tier-2 missing-disclosure defect, not a tier-1 surface telling a user something untrue. §17.2 of the vision also discloses the relabelling in canon.  Separately, and not part of R6.1: the honest surface has no working navigation link. CommandPalette.tsx:66 and SearchMeshModal.tsx:40 both route to /introspection, while App.tsx registers only /cognitive-introspection (:249), so both fall through to the catch-all at :257. The correct label is reachable only by typing the URL - a distinct dead-link defect I am flagging rather than folding into this verdict.
- **refuter's evidence:** I executed GET http://127.0.0.1:8086/api/v1/biometrics/status myself and got resource_flow 4.9, host_cpu_percent 95.1, resource_flow_basis "host CPU headroom (100 - cpu%) - spare capacity, not workload" - so the relabelling and the API-side basis both reproduce. I read app_mvp.py:523 and :599-601, useWorkstationBiometrics.ts:18-24 and :89-93, BiometricStatus.tsx in full, and Shell.tsx:296. I confirmed by my own grep that no component reads resource_flow_basis or host_cpu_percent. Then I ran my OWN independently chosen grep - for the VALUE `resource_flow` rather than the two field spellings the assessor searched - and that turned up a reader they missed: Introspection.tsx:107 renders the same number as a LoadVisual labelled "Host CPU headroom", on a route registered at App.tsx:249. I also checked for a test or guard over the cardio labelling (none exists) and traced the nav links to that honest page.

### R6.2 · The layer record built to tell the truth about the 7 layers still asserts an integral term the regulator denies, and asserts that an unreached layer "does not run anywhere" while its live surface is up — **DOC_OVERCLAIM** · tier 2

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** FU-307 corrected the PID wording in the comment block (quality.py:506-508) and left the served basis string 450 lines above it — one writer fixed, not every writer. It is tier 2 rather than tier 1 because the UI's layerTitle helper (apps/workstation-superapp/src/lib/api.ts:277-298) renders layer_states and layers_note but not layer_basis, so the integral-term sentence stops at the API; the "does not run anywhere" universal DOES render.
- **claim:** §8 (W512): "`geospheric/regulator.py` is not a controller: its `integral_error` is declared and never accumulated, its `dt` is discarded, its corrective actions are strings … and `vbs/quality.py`, the one surface built to tell the truth about these layers, currently calls that file 'a real PID regulator' (registered FU-307, Tier 1)."
- **observed:** Executed POST /api/v1/swarm/cascade {"mission":"R6 audit probe: layer basis reach"} → 200, and the response's `biomimetic.layer_basis.Endocrine` reads: "agentic_core/biomimicry/geospheric/regulator.py holds a real HomeostaticRegulator with an integral term and NOTHING IMPORTS IT…" (source: agentic_core/vbs/quality.py:57-58). The same file at :508 says the opposite — "once in __init__ as zeros and never accumulated, so there is no integral term" — and agentic_core/biomimicry/geospheric/regulator.py:13-14 says it too. Separately, `layers_note` in the same response asserts "CODE EXISTS BUT NOTHING CALLS IT — so the layer does not run anywhere: Respiratory, Endocrine", while §17.2 maps Respiratory to the Agent Hub and GET /api/v1/hub/agents returned a live platform roster (CEO/CFO/CTO/CMO, source "swarm roster (live platform agents)").
- **evidence:** quality.py:58 — "with an integral term and NOTHING IMPORTS IT" vs regulator.py:14 — "integral term; `dt` is computed in regulate() and never read, so there is no derivative term". And quality.py:37 — "a module exists and NOTHING IMPORTS IT, so the layer does not run anywhere" applied to Respiratory, measured only against molecular/triad_integration.py (quality.py:61-63).
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). BOTH HALVES REPRODUCED, on two routes, with my own inputs. (1) The Endocrine basis string served live asserts "a real HomeostaticRegulator with an integral term". The file it names denies that in its own docstring, and the code proves the denial: integral_error is bound once in __init__ as zeros (regulator.py:26) and never touched again; dt is computed at :42 and never read. quality.py's own comment at :506-514 states the correct measurement. So one file serves the untruth 450 lines above the line that corrects it - the FU-307 fix touched the comment writer and not the served string, exactly the "fix every writer, not one" class. (2) layers_note asserts a UNIVERSAL ("so the layer does not run anywhere") for Respiratory from a measurement of one module (molecular/triad_integration.py, quality.py:61-63), while the platform's own canon (SS17.2, vision :786-789/:804) names the Agent Hub as Respiratory's live surface and GET /api/v1/hub/agents returns a live 9-agent platform roster. The layer runs; the named module does not. That is a layer-level claim derived from module-level evidence.  CORRECTION TO ONE OF THEIR EVIDENCE LEGS (does not change the verdict): the assessor's tier rationale says the integral-term sentence "stops at the API" but the "does not run anywhere" universal "DOES render". That is wrong, and I refute it specifically. layerTitle (api.ts:277-298) destructures only layers, layers_declared, layer_states and self - it never reads layers_note OR layer_basis - and composes its own, narrower sentence: `code exists but nothing calls it: ${unreached.join(', ')}`. Grep for layers_note across apps/workstation-superapp/src returns zero hits. So BOTH offending strings stop at the API; the rendered tooltip carries only the weaker phrase (which is still a layer-level claim at odds with the live hub, but it does not assert the "does not run anywhere" universal or the integral term).  TIER STAYS 2, for the reason the assessor reached by a partly wrong route: these are two false assertions on an executed API surface that no rendered UI string repeats. Nothing fabricates a figure and nothing certifies what it could not assess - the record's own quality verdict is unaffected - so this is below the tier-1 bar of a reached rendered surface telling a user something untrue. It is above tier 3 because the surface is live, is reachable by any API consumer and by the probe path the platform itself documents, and is the one surface built expressly to tell the truth about these layers (the SS8/W512 clause), so an untruth here is self-refuting rather than a mere capability gap. This is not an AI_DISABLE_LOCAL artefact: both strings are static table text, identical under any model state.  WHY THE GUARD DID NOT CATCH IT (corroboration, not a separate finding): test_w506_p27_layers at test_mvp_spine.py:25298-25311 asserts only that the module NAMED in the basis has no importers, and its only basis-content assertions are `"NOTHING CALLS IT" in note` and `layer_basis["Endocrine"].strip()` (non-empty). No leg reads the basis adjectives and no leg checks the layer rather than the module - so both the "integral term" claim and the layer-level universal are structurally invisible to the one guard written to stop this rotting.

### R6.4 · The interceptor's self-healing branch calls a method that does not exist, so "Initiating Self-Healing" is logged and nothing heals — **STUB** · tier 3 *(assessed tier 2)*

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** The one healing path wired into the constitutional interceptor cannot execute, and the log line that precedes it states the opposite. The recirculation loop that drives the interceptor is paced and OFF by default (heartbeat.py:174 `self.auto_metabolic = False`; GET /api/v1/heartbeat/status → last_metabolic null, metabolic_basis "the loop is paced and OFF by default"), so no user-facing surface prints this today.

- **observed:** agentic_core/governance/uci_interceptor.py:82-84 logs "Definitive execution failed: … Initiating Self-Healing." then calls `await self.regulator.repair_tier({…}, tier="HDR")`. The branch therefore raises AttributeError in place of healing.
- **evidence:** uci_interceptor.py:83 — `output = await self.regulator.repair_tier({"error": str(e), "context": context}, tier="HDR")`; verified at runtime: "has repair: True | has repair_tier: False".
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). THE MECHANISM IS EXACTLY AS STATED — I reproduced it end-to-end, not just by listing methods. `Regulator` (agentic_core/change_control/regulator.py:19) defines `async def repair(self, fault, tier="HDR")`; `repair_tier` does not exist on the class or the bound instance. uci_interceptor.py:82 logs "Initiating Self-Healing." and :83 then raises AttributeError in its place. The branch is reachable by at least two different triggers (a failing action and a bad tech_id), and the pre-gates do not block it.  I CORRECT TWO THINGS, AND BOTH MATTER.  (1) THE TIER GOES DOWN TO 3, NOT 2. The M1 measure is a REACHED SURFACE that says something untrue. There is none here, and the gap is wider than the assessor measured. They noted the loop is off by default; I measured that `auto_metabolic` has no setter ANYWHERE outside the test file — no route, no config key, no CCA lever — so a user cannot turn it on even deliberately. The only non-test caller of `execute_cycle` is that gated heartbeat block, and the only non-test constructor of this interceptor is the recirculation orchestrator. Every live route uses a different class that has no repair call. And if the branch ever did fire, the surface that would report it tells the truth LOUDLY: heartbeat.py:421-429 catches it and records `last_metabolic = {"failed": "AttributeError: ...", "basis": "the metabolic cycle FAILED, so no latency was measured. A failure is not a clean beat"}` with action `metabolic_cycle_failed`. The untrue sentence ("Initiating Self-Healing") lives only in a `logger.error` in the server log, which no surface renders. Unreachable code whose only possible reporter discloses the failure by name is tier 3, not tier 2.  (2) THE FIX THE FINDING IMPLIES WOULD CREATE A TIER-1 DEFECT. "repair_tier -> repair" looks like a one-word rename, and that is the trap. I executed the correctly-named call: `repair(tier="HDR")` dispatches to `_homology_recovery`, which is `return {**fault, "repair_status": "recovered", "tier": "HDR", "template_used": self._template_cache.get(...) or {"baseline": "v-omega-nexus-master"}}` — a dict literal. Nothing is repaired; all four tiers (_fix_point_mutation / _reconcile_mismatch / _excise_damage / _homology_recovery) are one-line literals stamping "fixed"/"reconciled"/"excised"/"recovered". So renaming would turn a loud AttributeError into `{"status":"success","result":{"repair_status":"recovered"}}` returned from `intercept()` as the action's output — a fabricated healing claim travelling as data, with the log line now agreeing with it. That is the stronger reason STUB is the right word: the stub is the healing ITSELF, not the branch, and the broken name is the only thing currently stopping it from lying. Any fix must either repair for real or say plainly that no repair was performed and re-raise.  (3) ONE EVIDENCE CORRECTION. "grep for repair_tier repo-wide returns exactly this one call site" is true for live code but false repo-wide: _archive/agentic_core/constitutional/uci_v16.py:39 has `repair_corrupted_state({...}, repair_tier="HDR")` and uci.py:57 has `repair_state({...}, repair_tier="MMR")`. `repair_tier` was a KEYWORD ARGUMENT in both ancestors and was promoted to the method NAME during the v140 consolidation — which is the actual provenance of the defect and worth recording with the fix.  NET: the finding stands and is real and worth fixing, but it is not an M1 tier-1 item; its tier belongs at 3, which is why I call the direction MILDER.

### R6.5 · The six biogeochemical cycles exist and are honest, and no surface a person reads shows them — the "control surface" §8 asked for is API-only — **API_ONLY** · tier 3

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** The honesty discipline here is exemplary — an untuned gain is labelled a default, an unmeasured setpoint an aspiration, a coupling withheld rather than invented. The gap is reach: §8 asks for a control surface and there is no surface. Nothing untrue is told to anyone.
- **claim:** §8 (W484): "six named operational functions, each with a natural analogue — Water (liquidity), Carbon (growth and compounding), Nitrogen (risk transformation), Oxygen (metabolism), Phosphorus (allocation), Sulfur (resilience and error signalling) — with a coupling matrix between them. Its honest form here is a control surface over flows this platform already measures."
- **observed:** Executed GET /api/v1/cycles → 6 cycles, bound=1, total=6; only `oxygen` is assessable (measured_from "agentic_core/organism/heartbeat.py::OrganismHeartbeat.status"), the other five carry assessable=false with the reader that would bind them; every setpoint carries setpoint_is_aspiration=true; every gain carries gains_tuned=false; coupling_matrix is null with a stated reason. A repo-wide grep of apps/workstation-superapp/src for "/api/v1/cycles" returns no consumer (the only "biogeochemical" string in the UI is VSBEconomy.tsx's `cycle.biogeochemical_model`, which is the metabolism metaphor label from economy/metabolism.py:356).
- **evidence:** GET /api/v1/cycles → coupling_basis: "NOT REPORTED: the archived orchestrator holds a 6x6 coupling matrix and a Lyapunov bound, and a coupling between two cycles is only meaningful once BOTH are measured. 1 of 6 are bound today, so publishing a coupling would describe a relation between figures that do not exist".
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). THE FINDING STANDS AS VERDICT AND TIER, AND I CORRECT ITS EVIDENCE.  Reach (the substance of the finding) — REPRODUCED EXACTLY. There is no surface a person reads. A repo-wide grep, not just the UI grep the assessor ran, finds zero non-test non-doc consumers of /api/v1/cycles; and the UI contains none of the six cycle names, no coupling_matrix reader and no setpoint_is_aspiration reader. §8 asks for a control surface over flows the platform measures, and what exists is a JSON route only an API caller reaches. API_ONLY is right.  Tier 3 is right, and I checked the tier-1 candidates rather than accepting it. For a tier-1 defect there must be a reached surface saying something untrue. There is no reached surface at all here, so none can lie. The one place the UI touches this vocabulary (VSBEconomy.tsx:541 printing cycle.biogeochemical_model) renders a six-stage waterfall metaphor string from metabolism.py:356 — it neither names the six biogeochemical cycles nor claims any of them is controlled or measured, so it is not an overclaim standing in for the missing surface. And the one sentence that WAS a tier-1 lie — enriched_layers calling itself "Six-cycle PID-controlled homeostasis" while delegating to a shim consulting none of them — is already gone; line 75 is the comment recording its removal, not the claim. Tier stays 3.  THE CORRECTION, which runs MILDER on the machinery while leaving verdict and tier untouched: the assessor's "only oxygen is assessable" and "1 of 6 are bound" are artifacts of calling the route with no vsb_id, not properties of the platform. On both VSBs I chose myself, 3 of 6 bind — water to the sum of 7 waterfall pot balances, carbon to the cumulative posting count, oxygen to heartbeat beats — and the top-level basis and the coupling_basis both recompute to "3 of 6 are bound today", so those counts are derived from live state rather than written in as literals (the route's own docstring independently says "Three of six are in that state today", meaning unbound, which matches). The no-VSB response is not wrong either: each unbound cycle states "no VSB was named" as its reason. So the honesty discipline the assessor praised holds under an input they did not try, and it is deeper than they recorded: setpoint_is_aspiration is not a blanket literal but is justified per cycle by UNIT COMMENSURABILITY — water's setpoint is 75.0 degrees against a measurement in virtual WST, and the basis refuses the subtraction as "arithmetic over incommensurable units: real inputs, real division, a meaningless figure"; carbon and oxygen are aspirations because their modules declare no unit at all. For every bound cycle the deviation and homeostatic keys are ABSENT, not null and not zero, exactly as the docstring promises. Nothing here fabricates a figure.  I log the direction as SAME because the verdict label and the tier — which is the M1 measure — reproduce unchanged. The evidence correction is worth carrying forward on its own: anyone reading R6.5 as "5 of 6 cycles are unmeasurable" would be acting on the assessor's missing query parameter, and the real residual gap is 3 unbound cycles (nitrogen, phosphorus, sulfur, each naming the reader that would bind it) plus a withheld coupling matrix — behind no surface at all.

### R6.6 · Customer/user satisfaction, one of the four measures §8 says are continuously monitored, has no mechanism anywhere — **MISSING** · tier 3

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** Nothing claims it: the one place that could have fabricated it returns None with a reason, and no page prints a satisfaction figure. - **refutation: REFUTED — corrected to MISSING.** The premise as written — "has no mechanism anywhere", "no collector, no store, no route and no surface" — is wrong, and I refuted it with my own two tickets. There is an end-to-end user-written satisfaction signal: a store (data/support_tickets.json, concurrency-safe, strict read), a three-state confirmation that only a named user can write, an honest denominator (my unconfirmed ticket was excluded from both sides; the empty store refused to report 0.0 or 1.0), four live routes, and a page that renders the counts and the method rather than a percentage. The assessor's method caused the miss: they grepped the LITERAL "satisfaction", and this mechanism never uses the word — the memory lesson "grep the call, not the method name" exactly. They even quoted the comment two lines above the pointer to it and did not follow it.  Why MILDER and PARTIAL rather than REFUTED: the §8 measure itself is still not constituted. What exists is one narrow channel (support-resolution confirmation), it is not named as the satisfaction measure, and nothing consumes it — tickets.rate() has exactly ONE caller in the tree (agentic_core/api/support.py:215); no fitness, no board tier, no heartbeat beat and no composite reads it, so "continuously monitored, evaluated, and improved" is not met and the assessor's downstream point about selection survives: a fitness built today still could not draw a satisfaction term from anything. So: real machinery, undisclosed-in-the-finding, with a genuine shortfall against the clause — PARTIAL, not MISSING.  Tier stays 3, not corrected: no reached surface tells a user anything untrue about satisfaction. The one place that could fabricate it holds None with a stated reason; get_fabric_health omits it and discloses NOT ASSESSED; the SLA surface refuses a percentage when there is no denominator; and the canon itself discloses the gap in the same section — docs/WORKSTATION_IDBO_WHOLE_VISION.md:404 reads "customer/user satisfaction has no mechanism anywhere" with FU-311 registered (docs/WORKSTATION_IDBO_LIVING_PLAN.md:423). That register row's own text is now stale in two ways my run proves: it says the only occurrences are a heading and "a hardcoded 'user_satisfaction: 1.0'" (it is None with a reason since W543), and it says the fix needs "a real signal from real users, however small" — W541-W543 already built one. Code read: agentic_core/support/tickets.py (three-state CONFIRMED_RESOLVED/UNCONFIRMED, store_lock + atomic_write_json + read_json_strict, confirm() raises TypeError on a non-bool, rate() denominator = confirmations only), agentic_core/api/support.py (4 live routes: ask/confirm/sla/policy-change), and the reached surface apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/support/Support.tsx (the rate card has no percentage path at all when rate===null; it prints sla.basis and sla.method). I also checked the claim side: zero "satisfaction" paths in the live openapi (486 paths), no frontend string claiming the four measures are continuously monitored, and drad.get_fabric_health() (served to the AI CEO as tool check_qep_fabric_health) omits satisfaction entirely and returns health NOT ASSESSED with a basis.

### R6.7 · The organism cannot turn over — no apoptosis, autophagy, senescence or death — and the only "mitosis" in the tree fabricates a diversity figure from the wall clock — **PARTIAL** *(assessed MISSING)* · tier 3

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** Unreached: nothing calls mitosis_scale or meiosis_recombine, so the clock-derived diversity figure never reaches a reader or a store a reader sees. The capability gap is real and §8 states it. Were a surface ever wired to this module, the diversity_score would be a tier-1 fabricated figure on the spot.
- **claim:** §8 (W512): "It cannot turn over. No apoptosis, no autophagy, no mitosis, no senescence and no death … A system that cannot remove or replace its own parts cannot be said to heal."
- **observed:** A repo-wide grep for apoptosis|autophagy|mitosis|senescence (excluding _archive) matches exactly two files: integration_tests/test_mvp_spine.py and agentic_core/change_control/regulator.py, where `mitosis_scale` (line 84-91) copies a dict `count` times into an in-memory `_template_cache` and `meiosis_recombine` (line 79-82) sets `child["diversity_score"] = 0.35 + (0.05 * time.time() % 1)` — a number derived from the clock and then logged to the UEG as "diversity". GET /api/v1/organism/lifecycle returns only concept→prototype→commercialise ("lifecycle_stages_reached": [], "farthest_stage": "none"); there is no removal, replacement or death stage. Neither regulator method has a caller (the only importer, agentic_core/governance/uci_interceptor.py, calls `repair_tier` — see R6.4).
- **evidence:** change_control/regulator.py:81 — `child["diversity_score"] = 0.35 + (0.05 * time.time() % 1)`; regulator.py:9-10 docstring — "Features: 4-tier DNA repair (BER/MMR/NER/HDR) and PID-based homeostasis", whose BER tier returns the constant `{"repair_status": "fixed", "tier": "BER", "integrity": 1.0}` (line 41-42).
- **refutation: STOOD, verdict CORRECTED MISSING → PARTIAL.** Every mechanical observation reproduced, and one is WORSE than assessed: diversity_score = 0.35 + (0.05 * time.time() % 1) has range [0.35, 1.35), so the "score" can exceed 1.0 — it is not a normalized score, not merely a clock-derived one. I proved the clock dependence with my own inputs: two structurally different parent pairs produced 0.8209 and 0.8266, differing only by elapsed time. mitosis_scale is an in-memory dict copy. Neither method has any caller.  But the finding's HEADLINE PREMISE is refuted. The assessor grepped biology VOCABULARY (apoptosis|autophagy|mitosis|senescence) and concluded the capability is absent — the "grep the call, not the method name" trap. Real removal-and-replacement machinery exists, is wired, and runs: (1) the native-AI model lifecycle retires and reinstates a part of the live estate — I retired a model of my own naming and saw it subtracted from the active estate per model_resource.py:137-140 and orchestrator.py:317, then reinstated it; (2) the self-healer is SELF-TRIGGERED removal from service — record_failure fires automatically at 14+ live call sites in gateway.py and orchestrator.py:72, is_open gates serving at 8 sites, and the part is readmitted after recovery_timeout, which is apoptosis-shaped and reached; (3) governance.py:681 retire_heartbeat_holds_for_consumed_events, called from living_vsbs.py:762 and revenue.py:317, reclaims the system's own stale state — autophagy-shaped; (4) nine live DELETE routes remove projects, agents, compositions, swarms and listings; (5) _archive/agentic_core/ubiquitin/autophagy.py shows the capability was ARCHIVED, not never built. So "no removal, replacement or death stage" and "cannot remove or replace its own parts" are both too strong. MISSING -> PARTIAL, direction MILDER.  The residual gap is real and narrower: no senescence or aging marker on any part, no death (nothing is permanently destroyed by the organism's own decision — retire is operator-triggered and reversible), no turnover stage on the lifecycle surface, and no turnover for VSBs or projects as organism parts.  TIER STAYS 3, and this is the point that matters for M1. The clock-derived figure never reaches a reader: no route calls either method, and my own in-process call landed in the gitignored repo-local data\ueg_audit.log while the server's /api/v1/ueg/recent shows no regulator_meiosis event. Nor does any reached surface overclaim turnover — /organism/lifecycle states its stage vocabulary plainly and flags the known vocabulary issue; /organism/self-healing/status says "no circuit has carried a call yet — nothing measured, health unknown"; /organism/health-summary discloses "defaulted to 1.0, not a measurement" and "simulated, not a measurement of this platform". No surface tells a user something untrue, so this is a capability gap, not a tier-1 fabrication. The assessor's own tier reasoning was sound; only the breadth of the conclusion was wrong.
- **refuter's evidence:** Executed regulator.meiosis_recombine in-process on TWO independently chosen parent pairs ({a:1,name:'refuter-parent-A'}/{b:2,name:'refuter-parent-B'} -> diversity_score 0.8209272235631943; then {a:1}/{b:2} -> 0.8265812456607818), proving the figure is independent of parent content and tracks the wall clock only. Ran mitosis_scale({'t':'refuter-template'},3) -> 3 clones, _template_cache keys clone_0..clone_2. Read regulator.py end to end (line 81 formula, line 42 BER constant, lines 9-10 docstring). Grepped the repo for callers: only the two definitions match, and change_control/__init__.py:8-15 get_regulator never calls either. GET /api/v1/organism/lifecycle -> lifecycle_stages_reached [], farthest_stage 'none'. Then tested the CAPABILITY rather than the vocabulary: POST /api/v1/native-ai/lifecycle/retire with my own model name 'refuter-r67-probe-model' -> {"retired":["refuter-r67-probe-model"]}, and reinstate -> {"retired":[]}. Read model_resource.py:137-140, orchestrator.py:61/72/317, gateway.py:371-432/545-614 (14+ automatic record_failure sites), governance.py:681. Called /api/v1/organism/self-healing/status, /systems, /health-summary and /api/v1/ueg/recent. Confirmed my UEG writes went to the gitignored repo-local data\ueg_audit.log, not the server's store, and that git status stayed clean.

### R6.8 · Invariant 10 of the ten "absolute" architecture invariants has no implementation of any kind — **MISSING** · tier 3

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** Disclosed in the vision and awaiting an Owner ruling, and no surface claims a KPI gate exists. The word "absolute" in the clause heading is the only over-reach, and the status paragraph directly beneath it corrects the count.
- **claim:** §17.5: "The 10 architecture invariants (absolute). User isolation … · signal-bus atomicity · living business plan ≤5-min staleness · KPI gate before customer delivery."
- **observed:** A case-insensitive repo-wide grep for "KPI gate|kpi_gate|kpi gate" across agentic_core/ and apps/workstation-superapp/src/ (excluding _archive) returns ZERO lines. No delivery route consults a KPI: I executed POST /api/v1/swarm/delegate and POST /api/v1/swarm/cascade and both returned a §10 quality record (qms_gate_passed null, qms_basis "not assessable — floor-served") with no KPI term at all. Separately, the GaaS invariant is partial on the same count: 63 modules in agentic_core/api/ and only 8 reach a gaas intercept (board, constitutional_gaas, economy, forge, genesis, resource_fabric, swarm, transformation_orchestration), so §17.5's "true for 8 of 57 API modules" is now 8 of 63.
- **evidence:** grep -rn "kpi gate" -i agentic_core apps → no output. §17.5's own status note: "Not held — … KPI gate before customer delivery: nothing gates any delivery on KPIs (OWNER RULING P3.0 — build or amend)."
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). Reproduced as stated, in both halves, with my own inputs. (1) The absence is real and total: no KPI-gate implementation of any kind exists in code. The only KPI artifacts are a free-text objective field and a board.py placeholder string, and the one place a KPI value is read -- heartbeat.py:57 concatenating `o.get('kpi')` into the text handed to the compliance keyword screen -- is not a gate on anything. The two delivery gates that do exist, which I executed myself on a deliverable I invented, gate on section coverage, stub detection and a compliance screen; both disclose their own basis and the compliance screen says in words that it did not assess ("coverage: none", "not a certification"). (2) The tier is correctly 3, and I tested the tier claim rather than assuming it. No reached surface asserts a KPI gate exists, and none asserts the ten invariants hold: the canon text carrying the word "absolute" is never served to a user (only its path is), the frontend contains no invariant claim, and the one live surface that discusses Sec 17.5 (GET /api/v1/plan) discloses a shortfall instead of claiming compliance. Nothing fabricates a figure and nothing certifies what it could not assess, so this is not a tier-1 M1 defect. (3) The assessor's self-criticism is also right: the only over-reach is the word "absolute" in Sec 17.5's heading, and the status paragraph immediately beneath it (docs/WORKSTATION_IDBO_WHOLE_VISION.md:850) corrects it in plain terms -- "KPI gate before customer delivery: nothing gates any delivery on KPIs (OWNER RULING P3.0 -- build or amend)". (4) One small addition rather than a correction: the canon's GaaS figure "8 of 57 API modules" (:847) is stale by six modules -- I counted 63 -- and stale in the flattering direction (14% claimed against 12.7% actual). That is a doc-internal number on no reached surface, so it is a tier-3 doc staleness worth a one-line fix, not a lift in tier. Verdict MISSING / tier 3 stands unchanged; direction SAME.
- **refuter's evidence:** My own grep, not theirs: `grep -rniI -E "kpi[ _-]?gate"` over agentic_core/, apps/, docs/ excluding _archive -> zero hits in any .py/.ts/.tsx; every hit is in docs/ (the canon's own status text and the ledgers). A wider `\bkpi` grep over code returns only free text: the `kpi: str = ""` field at agentic_core/api/business_plan.py:333, the "TITLE | KPI | TIMELINE | OWNER_ROLE" parser at :91, prompt boilerplate, and the placeholder "(KPI to be set by the Board)" at agentic_core/api/board.py:464. No reader gates on a KPI. My own delivery route, independently chosen (NOT the assessor's swarm/delegate + swarm/cascade): POST /api/v1/deliverables/produce on the live :8086 with my own brief -- a tidal-turbine-array maintenance brief with sections [Scope, Schedule, Risks] -> deliv-a14f41e5, and zero occurrences of "kpi" in the entire response. Its quality_assurance gate is delivery_coverage 1.0 (basis "3 declared section(s) checked against the delivered content"), stub_found, substantive_length, plus a compliance screen returning not_assessable true / coverage "none" / "not a certification". Second independent route: POST /api/v1/vbs/qms/gate with my own figures (coverage 0.95, stubs_found false) -> passed true, min_coverage 0.95, honest basis string; its schema has exactly two fields, coverage and stubs_found. Tier check executed: GET /api/v1/plan returns zero "kpi" hits and volunteers the user-isolation invariant as "partial -- open on the control perimeter" (agentic_core/api/living_plan.py:94); frontend greps for "invariant" hit only two comments in BoardOfDirectors.tsx and "10 architecture|ten invariant|absolute" hit only Tailwind classes; the three modules naming the canon (cognition.py:76, integration_surface.py:231, transformation.py:189) serve only the file PATH, never Sec 17.5's text. GaaS half: 63 files in agentic_core/api/*.py, 20 mention gaas, and the same 8 the assessor named reach an intercept.

### R6.9 · The economic organism (§12) is delivered end-to-end: nine forms, the reserve-first waterfall, double-entry books with a period close, and every editorial input labelled as one — **DELIVERED**

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** Verified by execution rather than by reading: the arithmetic is real, the books balance, the virtual-WST boundary holds, and every editorial or simulated input is named as such on the surface that prints it. I found no truth defect in §12.
- **claim:** 
- **observed:** Executed, in order: GET /api/v1/economy/entity-types → 9 forms with per-form waterfalls, default waqf_ltd_hybrid; GET /api/v1/economy/waterfall → owner .20 / self_investment .30 / capital_fund .20 / user_projects .15 / charity .15, source "entity_template", note "Proportions of distributable profit (after reserves)"; POST /api/v1/economy/cycle {"revenue":1000} → reserve 200.0 off the top (reserve_rate_applied 0.2), distributable 800.0, circulation 160/240/160/120/120 exactly on the template, owner_accrual {accrued: true, amount_wst: 160.0}, five charity grants each carrying donation_100pct_verified "not_checked"; POST /api/v1/economy/close-period → trial_balance {debit 1000.0, credit 1000.0, balanced true}, balance_sheet balanced true, ueg_logged true; GET /api/v1/economy/owner-payments → real_money_rails "DISABLED", real_money_enabled false. The UI matches: VSBEconomy.tsx:595-601 prints that the five cause weights are "EDITORIAL CONSTANTS a maintainer typed… the 100%-donation flag is an eligibility rule reported as not_checked, never verified".
- **evidence:** POST /api/v1/economy/cycle → energy_state: "not_adjusted_term_unmeasured — the reserve was NOT adjusted: the metabolic term is a simulator on a constant load, floored so it cannot deplete, so it carries no evidence about this entity's energy" — the §8→§12 survival instinct declining to act on a figure it cannot trust, and saying so on the page (VSBEconomy.tsx:536-539).
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). Reproduced as stated, and under a form and numbers the assessor never used. The waterfall is genuinely form-sensitive (charity's 0.65 charity share and 0.0 owner share both applied, and the owner accrual correctly declined with a stated reason), the reserve is genuinely taken first off revenue at the requested rate with costs posted as a separate expense, and close-period is real double-entry computed from postings rather than mirrored from the request - it balanced on a fresh ledger with my 4000/1000/0.1 inputs AND carried prior-period retained earnings on the main ledger. An owner override that did not sum to 1.0 was normalised and reported normalised, so the "summing to 1.0" note is not a false claim. Real-money rails read DISABLED with zero paid out.  I attacked the disclosure gate specifically and it held. VSBEconomy.tsx:536 renders the energy caveat only when energy_state !== 'healthy', which looked like a route to printing "Metabolic Energy 100%" (tone="good") with no provenance. But metabolism.py:180 hard-sets _atp_measured = False, so the 'healthy' branch at line 192 is UNREACHABLE and the only live states are "not_adjusted_term_unmeasured - ... a simulator on a constant load, floored so it cannot deplete, so it carries no evidence" or "unavailable". The caveat is therefore unconditional in practice, and the unmeasured metabolic term declares itself on both the API and the page. The editorial labelling is real too: every charity grant carries weights_source "curated", donation_100pct_verified "not_checked", and a weights_provenance sentence; the venture positions carry funding_state "recorded_unfunded" with a basis saying the investee is NOT credited; the page prints the EDITORIAL CONSTANTS paragraph and a per-grant "score ... (editorial)" label.  The only defects I found are tier 3 and each discloses its own basis: (1) close-period's response header reports entity_type waqf_ltd_hybrid / platform_default even for a period whose cycles ran as charity - the P&L is nonetheless computed correctly from the postings and the note says "no form was requested"; (2) claimed_entity_type is null while entity_type_source is "caller_claimed", with the truth carried in form_note; (3) cash_flow.net_cash_movement reads 0.0 while the reserve_fund asset grew by the reserve, i.e. the reserve is counted as an operating payment. None is a reached surface telling a user something untrue, fabricating a figure, or certifying what it could not assess, so none is tier 1 or 2. Tier set to 3 to record those blemishes, not to contest the verdict. AI_DISABLE_LOCAL=1 is irrelevant here - §12 is arithmetic, and the one simulated input (the metabolic term) is named as simulated on the surface that prints it.
- **refuter's evidence:** Re-executed §12 with my OWN inputs, not the assessor's. GET /api/v1/economy/entity-types -> 9 forms, per-form waterfalls, default waqf_ltd_hybrid. POST /api/v1/economy/cycle {"revenue":2500} -> reserve 500 off the top (reserve_rate_applied 0.2), distributable 2000, circulation 400/600/400/300/300 exactly on template. INDEPENDENT FORM: POST /cycle {"vsb_id":"refuter-r69-probe","entity_type":"charity","revenue":4000,"costs":1000,"reserve_rate":0.1} -> reserve 400 (rate x revenue), costs posted separately, distributable 2600, circulation owner 0.0 / self 390 / capital 260 / projects 260 / charity 1690 (the charity template's 0.65), owner_accrual {accrued:false, amount_wst:0.0, note:"no owner share this cycle"} - the distributes_profit:false constraint is honoured in arithmetic. POST /close-period on that FRESH ledger -> income 4000, expenses 3600 (incl operating_costs 1000), net 400, balance_sheet reserve_fund 400 = equity 400 balanced:true, trial_balance 4000/4000 balanced:true, ueg_logged:true; on the main VSB after my 2500 cycle it carried prior state (retained 200 + current 500 = reserve_fund 700, trial balance 2700/2700). POST /waterfall with proportions summing to 2.0 -> normalised to .4/.2/.2/.1/.1 and REPORTED normalised, source owner_override; next cycle distributed 400/200/200/100/100 on 1000. GET /owner-payments -> my 400 accrual listed, paid_out 0.0, real_money_rails DISABLED. Read agentic_core/economy/metabolism.py:168-192 and apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/enterprise/VSBEconomy.tsx:536-539,595-601.
